TL;DR: 本文介绍了PSK团队在WMT 2026多语言指令共享任务上的提交方案：使用3.35B参数的Tiny Aya Global模型，为每个任务训练三个独立的QLoRA适配器（摘要、段落问答、开放问答）。实验表明上下文和摘要适配器优于多任务适配器，开放问答结果因答案长度和评估方法而异，最终提交了三个不同开放问答适配器的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多语言指令任务中，使用任务特定的独立适配器是否优于统一的多任务适配器，以提升摘要、问答等任务的表现。

Method: 基于3.35B参数的Tiny Aya Global模型，采用QLoRA训练三个独立适配器分别处理文档摘要、基于段落的问答和过滤后的独立问答任务。摘要数据包含科学论文及其作者撰写的摘要；同时训练了一个仅使用组织者提供数据的多任务适配器作为基线对比。

Result: 在留出集上，上下文和摘要适配器优于多任务适配器；开放问答结果表现不稳定，随答案长度和评估方法变化。最终提交了三个共享相同上下文和摘要适配器但使用不同开放问答适配器的系统。

Conclusion: 任务特定的独立QLoRA适配器在摘要和上下文问答上优于统一多任务适配器；开放问答的性能受评估方法和答案长度影响较大，需要通过多系统提交来覆盖不确定性。

Abstract: We describe the PSK submission to the WMT 2026 Multilingual Instruction Shared Task. Our system uses the 3.35B-parameter Tiny Aya Global model with three QLoRA adapters, one for each task. The adapters are trained on multilingual document-summary pairs, passage-based question answering, and filtered standalone question answering. The summarization data also includes scientific papers with their author-written abstracts. On our held-out split, the context and summarization adapters perform better than our multitask adapter, which was trained only on data supplied by the organizers. Results for open QA are mixed and vary with answer length and evaluation method. We therefore submit three systems with the same context and summarization adapters but different open-QA adapters.

</details>


### [46] [Tree-of-Concerns: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Debate for Unstated-Limitation Extraction in Scientific Critique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20777)
*Sahil Mishra,Niranjan Rajeev,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree-of-Concerns（ToC），一个多智能体框架，通过部署专家怀疑论者角色并以并行辩论树的形式从科学论文中提取未明确陈述的局限，相比最强基线在精确率上提升79%、覆盖率上提升11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科学文献量的增长，论文越来越多地低估/不充分报告其局限性，这掩盖了潜在的失败模式，亟需系统性方法来自动发现这些隐藏限制。

Method: ToC框架包含三个核心组件：（1）多个专家怀疑论者角色（skeptic personas），每个角色通过特定类别的分析透镜进行论证；（2）并行辩论树结构（parallel debate trees），让各角色独立进行结构化、基于证据的论证；（3）Panel Review机制，从五个视角重新评估每个存活的声明，以纠正类别漂移和严重程度校准错误。

Result: 在ToC-Bench基准（包含414篇研究论文、1,905个未陈述局限，数据来源于审稿人报告的弱点和后续引用批评）上，ToC相比最强基线在精确率上提升79%，覆盖率上提升11%，能够浮现出具体且基于证据的关切点。

Conclusion: ToC框架能有效支持审稿人进行系统性评估，通过多智能体辩论和面板复审机制发现论文中隐藏的局限性，为科学文献的批判性评估提供了自动化工具。

Abstract: As scientific literature grows and papers increasingly under-report limitations, multi-agent LLMs offer a promising approach to systematically uncover these hidden failure modes. Here, we introduce Tree-of-Concerns, a multi-agent framework that deploys specialized skeptic personas, each operating through a category-specific analytical lens, as parallel debate trees to extract unstated limitations from scientific papers. Each persona conducts structured, evidence-grounded argumentation, while a Panel Review mechanism re-evaluates each surviving claim from all five perspectives to correct category drift and severity miscalibration. Through experiments on ToC-Bench, our benchmark of 414 research papers with 1,905 unstated limitations, sourced from reviewer-reported weaknesses and follow-up citation critiques, we demonstrate that ToC improves precision by 79% and coverage by 11% relative to strongest baselines, surfacing specific, evidence-grounded concerns that support reviewers in systematic evaluation.

</details>


### [47] [Denoising the Future: Context-Aware Spectral Diffusion for Temporal Knowledge Graph Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20804)
*Yanglei Gan,Peng He,Run Lin,Peiyuan Jiang,Yifan Wang,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了FreqDiff，一种频率感知的扩散框架，用于时序知识图谱外推。通过查询槽去噪和双流去噪器（结合时间依赖建模与上下文感知的光谱校准），解决现有扩散方法中查询特定信号被无关历史事实稀释的问题，在四个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的时序知识图谱外推方法虽然通过生成式去噪提升了不确定性建模能力，但其对主题历史进行聚合条件化处理，难以充分区分查询特定证据与非显著的历史事实，导致目标判别性信号被稀释，从而影响外推性能。

Method: FreqDiff包含三个核心组件：(1) 将未来对象预测建模为查询槽去噪（query-slot denoising）；(2) 提出双流去噪器，集成时间依赖建模与上下文感知的光谱校准，其中光谱分支从可学习基函数中合成历史条件滤波器，自适应地重新校准去噪表示；(3) 引入频域正则化器，使去噪后的目标与真实对象在频谱空间中对齐。

Result: 在四个公开的时序知识图谱基准数据集上，FreqDiff达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能表现。

Conclusion: 频率感知的扩散框架能够有效缓解历史信息聚合带来的信号稀释问题，通过在频域中校准去噪表示并加入频谱对齐正则化，显著提升时序知识图谱外推的预测准确性。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graph (TKG) extrapolation seeks to infer future facts from time-varying relational histories. Recent diffusion-based approaches improve uncertainty modeling through generative denoising, but their aggregated conditioning on subject histories may insufficiently distinguish query-specific evidence from non-salient historical facts, thereby diluting target-discriminative signals. To bridge this gap, we propose FreqDiff, a Frequency-aware Diffusion framework for TKG extrapolation. Specifically, FreqDiff formulates future object prediction as query-slot denoising and develops a dual-stream denoiser that integrates temporal dependency modeling with context-aware spectral calibration. The spectral branch synthesizes history-conditioned filters from learnable bases to adaptively re-calibrate denoising representations, while a frequency-domain regularizer is proposed to align the denoised target with the gold object in spectral space. Experiments on four public TKG benchmarks demonstrate that FreqDiff achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [48] [STAR-OPD: Structured Aspect-Cascade-Aware On-Policy Reward Distillation for ABSA Quadruple Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20831)
*Tong Sun,Mingyang Ma,Jiayang Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-OPD，一种基于结构化级联感知的在线策略奖励蒸馏方法，用于解决情感分析四元组提取任务中学生模型在目标-方面接口处产生结构无效状态的问题。通过在学生轨迹上训练并应用集合结构奖励，STAR-OPD在E-ABSA20K和SemEval-2014上超越了离策略和通用在线策略基线，显著缩小了学生与教师之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型链式思维（CoT）模型在方面级情感分析（ABSA）四元组提取任务上表现优异，但将其蒸馏到小型可部署模型时存在特定失效模式：学生模型在目标-方面接口处产生结构无效状态（如目标-方面绑定断裂和幻觉目标），这些状态会污染下游预测。传统的离策略蒸馏只训练教师生成的轨迹，无法对推理时占主导的学生诱导结构状态提供足够监督，导致蒸馏效果不佳。

Method: STAR-OPD建立在通用在线策略蒸馏基础上，并将其具体化为ABSA四元组提取场景：(1) 在学生的实际采样轨迹（rollouts）上训练而非教师轨迹；(2) 设计级联感知的集合结构奖励，直接针对绑定一致性、目标接地（grounding）和细粒度方面消歧三个维度；(3) 通过结构化奖励惩罚断裂的目标-方面绑定和幻觉目标，纠正学生诱导的结构错误。

Result: 在E-ABSA20K和SemEval-2014数据集上，STAR-OPD一致优于离策略和通用在线策略基线，减少了目标幻觉现象，并显著提升了结构困难样本的性能。使用Qwen3-4B时，STAR-OPD大幅缩小了学生-教师差距，同时提高了推理效率。

Conclusion: 在线策略的结构化纠正在蒸馏ABSA四元组提取任务中至关重要。通过直接针对学生结构错误设计奖励机制，可以有效缓解学生-教师差距，验证了针对任务特定失效模式设计在线策略蒸馏方法的必要性。

Abstract: Aspect-based sentiment analysis (ABSA) quadruple extraction requires jointly predicting target, aspect, opinion, and sentiment over reviews that often contain multiple fine-grained sentiment tuples. While large chain-of-thought (CoT) models perform well on this task, distilling them into smaller deployable models remains difficult. We identify a task-specific failure mode in distilled ABSA extraction: student errors at the target-aspect interface create structurally invalid states, such as broken target-aspect bindings and hallucinated targets, which then corrupt downstream predictions. Conventional off-policy distillation is poorly suited to this setting because it trains only on teacher-generated trajectories and provides little supervision on the student-induced structural states that dominate inference. To address this mismatch, we propose STAR-OPD (STructured Aspect-cascade-aware On-Policy Reward Distillation), which builds on generic on-policy distillation and instantiates it for ABSA quadruple extraction with cascade-aware, set-structured rewards. STAR-OPD trains on student rollouts and applies set-structured rewards that directly target binding consistency, target grounding, and fine-grained aspect disambiguation. Experiments on E-ABSA20K and SemEval-2014 show that STAR-OPD consistently outperforms off-policy and general on-policy baselines, reduces target hallucination, and substantially improves performance on structurally hard cases. With Qwen3-4B, STAR-OPD substantially narrows the student-teacher gap while improving inference efficiency, highlighting the importance of on-policy structural correction for distilled ABSA extraction.

</details>


### [49] [Ontology-Driven Structural Regularization for Document-Level Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20856)
*Laura Menotti,Stefano Marchesin,Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出本体驱动的框架来量化和强制文档级关系抽取（DocRE）数据集中的结构一致性，发现DocRED distant数据集中存在大量结构噪声（包括本体约束违反和逻辑矛盾），并在训练中强制执行结构良构性可显著减少逻辑矛盾并提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DocRE严重依赖昂贵的人工标注数据，而大规模远程监督资源（如DocRED distant）因噪声问题未被充分利用。作者发现一个被忽视的关键噪声来源——关系三元组中的结构不一致性，包括本体约束违反和逻辑矛盾。

Method: 提出一个本体驱动的框架，用于量化和强制DocRE数据集中的结构一致性；在训练过程中强制执行结构良构性（structural well-formedness）作为结构正则化手段。

Result: 分析揭示了DocRED distant中存在大量结构噪声，且这些不一致性会传播到模型预测中；训练中强制执行结构良构性显著减少了逻辑矛盾，并持续提升了泛化性能。

Conclusion: 结构一致性是DocRE中一个被忽视的监督轴，结构正则化是利用大规模远程监督数据的有效策略。这些发现为DocRE提供了一种新的数据质量改善方向。

Abstract: Document-Level Relation Extraction (DocRE) relies heavily on costly manually annotated datasets, while large distant supervision resources such as DocRED distant remain underexploited due to noise. We show that a critical yet overlooked source of noise lies in structural inconsistencies within relational triples, including violations of ontology constraints and logical contradictions.
  We introduce an ontology-driven framework to quantify and enforce structural consistency in DocRE datasets. Our analysis reveals substantial structural noise in DocRED distant and demonstrates that such inconsistencies propagate to model predictions. Enforcing structural well-formedness during training significantly reduces logical contradictions and consistently improves generalization performance. These findings establish structural consistency as a missing axis of supervision in DocRE and highlight structural regularization as an effective strategy for leveraging distant data at scale.

</details>


### [50] [KREL: Automatic Medical Coding via Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20887)
*Xubin Chen,Yipeng Zhou,Wen Sun,Chengkai Huang,Xiaoming Fu,Quan Z. Sheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出KREL框架，将外部ICD编码指南作为结构化知识，与LLM的临床文本理解和推理能力紧密结合，有效解决自动医疗编码中临床笔记过长、ICD标签空间庞大及编码规则复杂等挑战，在基准数据集上优于现有PLM和LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动医疗编码（AMC）对医疗报销、质量报告和临床研究至关重要，但现有方法面临三大挑战：临床笔记过长难以有效解读、ICD标签空间巨大、复杂编码规则未被LLM显式捕获，导致幻觉和不合规问题。

Method: 提出KREL（Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs）框架，将外部ICD编码指南编码为结构化知识，与LLM的临床文本理解与推理能力深度耦合，实现领域知识与LLM推理的紧密结合。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，KREL在多个指标上一致优于强PLM基线以及最先进的LLM基线方法，降低了幻觉并提升了编码标准的合规性。

Conclusion: 将外部编码指南作为结构化知识融入LLM推理流程，能够显著改善自动医疗编码的性能和合规性，为AMC任务提供了一种有效且可落地的知识引导推理方案。

Abstract: Automatic Medical Coding (AMC), which assigns standardized International Classification of Diseases (ICD) codes to clinical notes, is essential for medical reimbursement, quality reporting, and clinical research. Existing pre-trained language model (PLM)-based methods typically formulate AMC as an extreme multi-label classification problem over a predefined code set, while recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches instead frame it as generation or multi-step reasoning. However, key challenges remain, including the extreme length of clinical notes that hinders effective interpretation, the vast ICD label space, and complex coding rules that are not explicitly captured by LLMs. In this work, we propose Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs (KREL), a framework that leverages LLMs for clinical text understanding and reasoning while integrating external ICD coding guidelines as structured knowledge. This design enables tight coupling between domain knowledge and LLM reasoning, reducing hallucinations and improving compliance with coding standards. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that KREL consistently outperforms strong PLM-based and state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [51] [ForeDreamer: A Self-Evolving Dual-Agent Memory Architecture for Future Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20920)
*Linhao Zhong,Zongze Du,Linyu Wu,Yu Bo,Hourong Li,Chenchen Jing,Hao Chen,Yuling Xi,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ForeDreamer提出一种自进化的双智能体框架，将开放网络证据转化为结构化记忆后再进行预测，分离事实记忆与经验记忆，在Prophet Arena和FutureX上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放的未来事件预测需要从嘈杂、冗余且不完整的网络证据中提取可靠信号，而现有检索/记忆机制要么直接将检索结果喂给智能体，要么仅采用简单记忆功能（如存储和重用先前信息），不足以支持高质量的开放网络预测。

Method: ForeDreamer是一个自进化的双智能体框架，将记忆分为事实记忆（当前预测所需的问题特定证据状态）和经验记忆（跨预测场景积累的持久性智能体经验）。主智能体负责搜索与预测，记忆处理子智能体使用专用工具将搜索结果转化为事实记忆。经验记忆通过双轨机制进化，同时改进预测决策和事实记忆构建。

Result: 在Prophet Arena和FutureX两个基准上的实验证明了ForeDreamer的有效性。

Conclusion: 将原始网络证据转化为结构化记忆后再进行推理，相比直接使用嘈杂检索结果能显著提升预测性能；双轨经验记忆进化机制有效增强了预测决策与记忆构建两个环节。

Abstract: Open-web future event prediction requires agents to distill reliable signals from noisy, redundant, and incomplete evidence. Existing retrieval/memory mechanisms directly feed retrieved information to agents or rely on simple memory functions such as storing and reusing prior information for prediction, leaving them insufficient for open-web forecasting. We propose to transform raw web evidence into structured memory before prediction, enabling agents to reason over distilled, question-specific evidence rather than noisy retrieval results. This paper presents ForeDreamer, a self-evolving dual-agent framework for managing memory over open-web evidence. ForeDreamer separates factual memory, a question-specific evidence state for the current forecast, from experiential memory, persistent agent experience accumulated across forecasting episodes. It uses a main agent for search and prediction, and a memory-processing subagent to convert search results into factual memory with dedicated tools. ForeDreamer further evolves experiential memory through two tracks, improving both forecasting decisions and factual-memory construction. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX demonstrate the effectiveness of ForeDreamer. Project page: https://zhongzero.github.io/ForeDreamer

</details>


### [52] [Source-Free MT Evaluation Is Not MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20925)
*Baban Gain,Ramakrishna Appicharla,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证机器翻译评价中充分性（adequacy）必须基于源文判断，而非以参考译文为标准。参考译文只是源文的一种可能表达，可能存在偏见、欠具体化或错误。作者呼吁将质量估计（QE）重新定位为源文锚定的充分性评价的主要方法，而非参考译文不可用时的备选方案；同时呼吁设计混合指标时明确优先考虑源文-译文忠实度，参考译文仅作为辅助证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于参考译文的评价指标（reference-based metrics）在机器翻译评价中占据标准地位，原因之一是质量估计方法（QE）与人工判断的相关性通常较低。然而，无源文、仅依赖参考译文的评价在定义上不忠实于翻译充分性的概念，对那些输出保留了源文含义但与参考译文不同的系统不公平。参考译文只是源文的一种可能翻译，可能引入偏差、欠具体化或错误。

Method: 论文采用论证性分析方法，通过逻辑推理证明：(1) 充分性必须相对于源文来判断；(2) 源文-参考译文-假设译文三元评价只有在裁判将参考译文视为辅助证据而非主要标准时才公平；(3) 通过实证观察指出现有混合指标高度依赖参考译文而非源文；(4) 任何去除源文或允许参考译文主导源文的评价协议在结构上都不完整的。

Result: 论文指出现有混合指标（hybrid metrics）相比源文更高度依赖参考译文。并且观察到现有MT论文普遍偏好基于参考译文的指标，仅在参考译文不可用时才使用QE指标。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁将QE重新定位为源文锚定的充分性评价的主要方法，而非因缺少参考译文才使用的备选方案；同时呼吁设计混合指标时在结构上明确优先考虑源文-假设译文忠实度，仅将参考译文用作互补证据。

Abstract: Reference-based metrics remain the standard choice in machine translation evaluation, partly because quality estimation methods often correlate less well with human judgments. As a result, source-free, reference-based evaluation has become the practical norm, even though it is unfaithful to the definition of translation adequacy and unfair to systems whose outputs preserve the source meaning while differing from the reference. This paper argues that adequacy must be judged with respect to the source. A reference is only one possible rendering of the source and may introduce bias, under-specification, or errors. We further argue that source-reference-hypothesis evaluation is fair only when the judge treats the reference as auxiliary evidence rather than as the primary standard. Otherwise, even source-aware evaluation can reduce adequacy to preference towards reference. We show the existing hybrid metrics are highly reliant on reference compared to source. Our argument is not that all automatic MT metrics fail to use the source. Rather, we argue that any evaluation protocol that removes the source, or allows the reference to dominate the source, is structurally incomplete for adequacy evaluation. However, existing MT papers generally prefer reference-based metrics and use QE metrics only when reference is unavailable. We therefore call for QE to be reframed as a primary approach to source-grounded adequacy evaluation, rather than as a fallback motivated by missing references. We further call for hybrid metrics whose designs explicitly prioritize source--hypothesis faithfulness while using references only as complementary evidence.

</details>


### [53] [MentorPulse: Refreshing Cross-Model Latent Guidance for Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20927)
*Ziwu Liu,Guozhong Li,Chen Qiu,Weiyang Kong,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MentorPulse，通过带容量限制的槽记忆和增量刷新机制，让冻结的大模型导师在长程生成中保持对小型学生的动态指导，在13个数据集上平均缩小52.2%的师生差距，并在11对模型组合上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨模型潜在引导方法在长程生成中失效，因为静态引导信号在输出不断增长时逐渐过时。实验表明，在多轮指令跟随中，静态引导反而使4B学生的约束满足度比无引导基线低2.5个点，这揭示了对动态引导机制的需求。

Method: MentorPulse包含三个核心组件：(1) 将导师状态压缩为有容量限制的槽记忆；(2) 增量处理新生成的token并更新学生读取的记忆；(3) 通过门控交叉注意力更新记忆，且不重置学生的KV缓存。另提出Windowed Refresh Training将桥接暴露于前缀条件记忆的训练中。

Result: 在13个数据集上，MentorPulse平均缩小了52.2%的师生性能差距，优于C2C、T2T和等预算LoRA，在长输出上收益最大。它在来自三个模型族的11对师生组合上全部取得最佳表现，且优势随能力差距增大而缩小。轻量级的读模式检查可在部署前预测增益，成本测量识别出在长输出上优于文本引导的刷新间隔。

Conclusion: MentorPulse证明了训练无关的引导刷新机制能够有效维持跨模型引导在长程生成中的有效性，且其读模式检查为实际部署提供了可预测的性能依据，表明动态刷新引导是优于静态引导和传统微调方案的可行路径。

Abstract: Cross-model latent guidance lets a frozen large mentor encode an input once and a frozen small student generate from the resulting signal. Existing methods keep this signal fixed, assuming it stays useful as the output grows; we show this fails in long-form generation. On multi-turn instruction following, static guidance pushes a 4B student's constraint satisfaction 2.5 points below its no-guidance baseline; a training-free refresh every 16 tokens changes only the memory content and restores a 2.0-point gain over that baseline. We propose MentorPulse to keep guidance fresh at practical cost: it compresses mentor states into a capped slot memory, incrementally processes newly generated tokens, and updates the memory that the student reads through gated cross-attention without resetting the student's KV cache. Windowed Refresh Training exposes the bridge to prefix-conditioned memory. Across thirteen datasets, MentorPulse closes 52.2% of the mentor-student gap on macro average, outperforming C2C, T2T, and equal-budget LoRA, with the largest gains on long outputs. It performs best on all eleven mentor-student pairs from three model families, with margins that narrow as the capability gap grows, and a lightweight read-pattern check predicts the gain before deployment. Measured costs identify refresh intervals that dominate text guidance on long outputs.

</details>


### [54] [Quantization-Aware Healing: A Practical Recipe for Recovering Compressed, 4-Bit LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20953)
*Bakbergen Ryskulov,Iker García-Ferrero,David Montero,David Jansen,Ali Hashemi,Jezabel R. Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出量化感知修复（QAH）方法，直接从未压缩的原始模型蒸馏4-bit量化学生模型，解决了压缩+量化后模型需要"修复"阶段但标准QAT收敛慢且易崩溃的问题。在GPT-OSS 120B→60B→MXFP4管线中，QAH学生模型在7/9个基准上达到或超过其bfloat16源模型，内存仅为1/4，参数量减半，性能比QAT快约7倍且训练稳定，已开源为Hypernova-60B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型低成本部署需要同时进行结构压缩（参数裁剪）和4-bit量化，但这会显著损害推理、数学、编码和长上下文能力，因此部署前需要"修复"（healing）阶段。默认的量化感知训练（QAT）配方在压缩+量化的模型中收敛缓慢且超过峰值后崩溃，需要手动的early stopping和长时间超参数搜索，不可复用。需要一个无需多周超参数搜索、稳定可靠的修复配方。

Method: 采用量化感知修复（QAH）替代QAT。核心洞察：结构压缩后的模型从未在完整精度下独立训练过，其bfloat16检查点本质上是蒸馏恢复的近似版本；因此QAH直接从未压缩的原始教师模型蒸馏4-bit学生模型，而非像QAT那样用硬标签重新拟合压缩量化模型。在GPT-OSS 120B→60B→MXFP4管线中实施，将QAH与匹配的QAT基线进行对比测试。

Result: QAH学生模型在7/9个基准上匹配或超过其bfloat16源模型，权重内存减少约4倍，参数量仅为教师的一半，以开放权重发布为Hypernova-60B。与QAT基线相比，达到可比峰值速度约快7倍，且在持续训练下保持稳定，无需手动早停。还发现分布式训练后端之间存在大型且可复现的质量差距，并报告了部署经验。

Conclusion: QAH是一种无需多周超参数搜索即可部署的修复配方，相比QAT在压缩+量化模型上更快速、更稳定，同时保持甚至提升模型质量。论文还提供了分布式训练后端质量差距等部署教训，为低成本LLM部署提供了一套可复用的方法。

Abstract: Serving large language models cheaply increasingly means shipping models that are both structurally compressed to a fraction of their parameters and quantized to 4 bits. Together these steps degrade reasoning, mathematics, coding, and long-context behavior enough to require a recovery, or healing, stage before deployment. The default recipe, quantization-aware training (QAT), re-fits the compressed, quantized model to hard labels; in our pipeline it converged slowly and collapsed past its peak. We adopted Quantization-Aware Healing (QAH) instead. Because a structurally compressed model is never independently trained at full precision, its bfloat16 checkpoint is a distillation-recovered approximation of the original; QAH distills the 4-bit student directly from the original, uncompressed model. On a GPT-OSS 120B to 60B to MXFP4 pipeline, the QAH student matches or beats its bfloat16 source on 7 of 9 benchmarks at roughly 4 times less weight memory and half the teacher's parameter count, and is released open-weight as Hypernova-60B. Against a matched QAT baseline it reaches a comparable peak about 7 times faster and stays stable under continued training, without hand-tuned early stopping. We also report deployment lessons, including a large, reproducible quality gap between distributed-training backends. Our aim is a recipe deployable without a multi-week hyper-parameter search.

</details>


### [55] [Extractive Summarization for Arabic Documents Using SAraBERT with a Semantic Siamese Similarity Evaluation Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20964)
*Sami Shames El Deen,Mariette Awad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAraBERT，一种在AraBERT基础上引入句间Transformer层用于抽取式摘要的增强模型，并提出Semantic Siamese Similarity这一新评估指标来衡量文本间的语义相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有阿拉伯语抽取式摘要模型在捕获文档主旨方面存在不足，需要一种既能提升摘要覆盖率又能更准确地评估摘要与原文语义相似度的方案。

Method: 在AraBERT架构中加入句间Transformer层以增强跨句语义建模能力；同时提出Semantic Siamese Similarity评估指标，通过Siamese网络结构度量两个文本输入之间的语义相似度。

Result: 使用BLEU、ROUGE和Semantic Siamese Similarity在SAraBERT及已有相关模型上进行验证，仿真结果表明所提模型具有有效性。

Conclusion: SAraBERT在阿拉伯语抽取式摘要任务上表现优于基线，提出的Semantic Siamese Similarity指标为摘要评估提供了新的视角，并激励了后续研究。

Abstract: In this research, we introduce SAraBERT, an enhanced version of AraBERT which proposes inter-sentence transformer layers for extractive summarization tasks. To ensure that the summaries generated by SAraBERT achieve a high coverage of the document's main ideas, we propose Semantic Siamese Similarity, a novel evaluation metric that measures the level of similarity between two text inputs. We validated using BLEU, ROUGE, and Semantic Siamese similarity on Sarabert and published related models. Simulation results showed the effectiveness of our proposed model and motivate follow on research.

</details>


### [56] [Target-Aware Calibration Data Selection for Preserving Uncertainty in Quantized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21019)
*Zhen Yang,Sizai Hou,Kaiwen Zheng,Yaofang Liu,Liang He,Yixuan Chen,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Doub-Preserving Quantization (DPQ)框架，将量化校准数据选择形式化为目标依赖的不确定性保持问题，通过构造高怀疑样本与通用锚点的混合校准集，在不同部署目标（如SQuAD2可回答性边界、多项选择QA行为）下自适应选择最优校准配方，实验覆盖8个语言模型、9个NLP基准和22种对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化广泛用于部署大语言模型，但其对不确定性行为（如置信度、边际、弃权）的影响很少被作为首要目标考虑。不同部署场景关注输入分布的不同区域，而先前工作主要优化面向准确率的压缩指标或在量化后调整分数，未能解决校准数据选择与部署目标不匹配的问题。

Method: 将校准数据选择形式化为目标依赖的不确定性保持问题，提出分布保持风险和边界保持风险两种形式化目标；通过混合错配论证说明单一校准方案无法适配所有目标；提出DPQ框架，利用全精度预测构造目标对齐的校准混合集，包含高怀疑示例（如DPQ-r75、DPQ-r50）和通用锚点，并探索confidence-only、entropy-only等单信号变体。

Result: 在8个语言模型、9个NLP基准和22种对比方法上的实验表明，领先的固定配方随保持目标而改变：DPQ-r75在SQuAD2可回答性边界保持上最佳，而更温和或单信号的变体（DPQ-r50、confidence-only、entropy-only）在多项选择QA行为保持上表现更好。

Conclusion: 校准数据应针对部署所需保持的特定全精度分数行为来选择，而非作为固定的量化细节处理。不存在普遍最优的校准配方，应根据目标依赖地选择量化校准方案。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to deploy large language models, but its effect on uncertainty behavior, such as confidence, margins, and abstention, is rarely treated as a primary objective. We frame calibration-data selection for quantization as a target-dependent uncertainty-preservation problem. Different deployments emphasize different regions of the input distribution, yet prior work mainly optimizes accuracy-oriented compression metrics or adjusts scores after quantization. We formalize this goal with distributional and boundary preservation risks, and provide a simple mixture-mismatch argument explaining why no single calibration recipe should be expected to fit all targets. We introduce Doubt-Preserving Quantization (DPQ), a lightweight pre-quantization recipe family that uses full-precision predictions to construct target-aligned calibration mixtures of high-doubt examples and generic anchors. Across 8 language models, 9 NLP benchmarks, and 22 comparison methods, the leading fixed recipe changes with the preservation target: DPQ-r75 leads on SQuAD2 answerability-boundary preservation, while milder or single-signal variants, including DPQ-r50, confidence-only, and entropy-only, better preserve broad multiple-choice QA behavior. These results show that calibration data should be selected for the specific full-precision score behavior a deployment needs to preserve, rather than treated as a fixed quantization detail.

</details>


### [57] [Free-Text Evaluation of LLMs for 5G Domain Knowledge and Fault Analysis using LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21021)
*Rishiraj Sengupta,Sotiris Chatzimiltis,Mohammad Shojafar,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了三个轻量级边缘可部署LLM（Claude-Haiku-4.5、GPT-5.4-Mini、Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite）在5G/6G网络自由文本故障诊断中的表现。所有模型在故障诊断上达到90%以上准确率，但对3GPP和O-RAN规范的零样本召回率低于60%。采用多裁判LLM评分框架，裁判间一致性达0.90以上。Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在性能、成本和延迟间取得最佳平衡，最适合生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有5G故障分析基准依赖固定答案的多项选择题（MCQ），无法真实反映实际网络诊断中自由文本场景。作者旨在探究轻量级、边缘可部署的LLM是否具备深度自由文本诊断能力，并建立可扩展的评分验证框架。

Method: 1) 将评估从MCQ范式转向自由文本生成范式；2) 在三个基准（TeleQNA ORAN FT、5G-Faults FT、TeleInter FT）上评估三个轻量级LLM；3) 使用三个独立前沿裁判LLM对输出进行评分，并计算两两裁判间一致性作为LLM-as-Judge方法论的经验检验。

Result: 所有三个模型在故障诊断上均达到至少90%的准确率；但零样本3GPP和O-RAN规范召回率均低于60%，是关键短板；多裁判评分的一致率在所有运行中均达0.90以上，验证了LLM-as-Judge评分框架的可靠性和可复现性；Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在推理成本和延迟方面最优，兼具竞争力的准确率。

Conclusion: 轻量级边缘LLM已具备生产级5G故障诊断能力，但需要对领域规范知识的记忆能力进行增强（如检索增强或微调）。多裁判LLM评分框架可可靠地应用于开放文本评估，为电信领域大规模自动化评测提供了可行路径。Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite是当前生产部署的最佳候选。

Abstract: Real-world fault analysis in 5G and emerging 6G networks demands domain expertise to analyze free-text diagnostics, including root-cause explanations and recommended actions. LLMs have emerged as a promising approach to automating this, yet whether lightweight, edge-deployable models are capable of performing in-depth free-text diagnostics remains an open question. While existing benchmarks rely on restrictive MCQs with fixed answer keys, this paper evaluates 5G domain understanding and fault analysis in a free-text generation format. Transitioning to this paradigm requires evaluating lightweight, edge-deployable AI models on open-ended diagnostic reasoning, alongside a dependable framework to validate these text outputs at scale. To address this we evaluate three lightweight LLMs, Claude-Haiku-4.5, GPT-5.4-Mini, and Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite, on free-text 5G domain knowledge and fault-analysis tasks across three benchmarks, TeleQNA ORAN FT, 5G-Faults FT, and TeleInter FT. Three independent frontier judges score outputs, and pairwise inter-judge agreement is measured as an empirical test of the LLM-as-Judge methodology. All three models reach at least 90% accuracy on fault diagnosis, while zero-shot recall of 3GPP and O-RAN specifications remains the critical gap, with all models scoring below 60%. Mean inter-judge agreement is at least 0.90 across all runs, indicating that multi-judge LLM scoring produces consistent, reproducible grades for open-ended telecom responses. Operationally, Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite offers the best efficiency trade-off, combining competitive accuracy with the lowest inference cost and latency, making it the most suitable candidate for production telecom deployments.

</details>


### [58] [Scaling Unsupervised Word Alignment to Documents via Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21023)
*Michelle Wastl,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CTFAlign和MDPAlign两种无需训练的轻量级文档级词对齐方法，通过粗到细的精炼策略（CTFAlign）和主对角线位置约束（MDPAlign）直接处理全文文档，无需句子分割，将词对齐错误率从0.412降至0.326，并显著提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统词对齐研究聚焦于句子层面，但许多跨语言任务需要文档级对应关系。虽然多语言嵌入模型能编码长输入，但直接将句子级对齐算法应用于文档会导致性能退化，需要专门针对文档级词对齐的方法。

Method: 提出两种方法：(1) CTFAlign，采用粗到细的精炼策略，将对齐搜索空间限制在语义相似区域；(2) MDPAlign，一种更简单的替代方案，通过主对角线先验约束对齐位置。两种方法均直接作用于完整文档，不依赖句子分割或句子对齐。

Result: 在六种语言对上（覆盖不同语系距离、资源丰富度和文档长度）评估，跨三个模型平均，CTFAlign将词对齐错误率从0.412降至0.326。下游任务（文档级翻译覆盖率评估和语义差异识别）也获得改进。

Conclusion: 文档级词对齐需要专门设计的方法，直接套用句子级算法会性能下降。CTFAlign和MDPAlign提供了有效的轻量级解决方案，方法开源并可供复现。

Abstract: Word alignment has traditionally been studied between sentences, but many cross-lingual tasks increasingly require correspondences across full documents. While recent multilingual embedding models can encode long inputs, we show that applying algorithms designed for sentences directly to documents leads to performance degradation. To address this, we introduce CTFAlign, a lightweight, training-free approach for document-level word alignment. CTFAlign applies a coarse-to-fine refinement strategy that restricts the alignment search space to semantically similar regions. Additionally, we introduce MDPAlign, a simpler alternative that constrains alignments by position with a main diagonal prior. Both approaches operate directly on full documents without relying on sentence segmentation or sentence alignment. We evaluate these methods across six language pairs varying in typological distance, resourcedness, and document length. Averaged over three models, CTFAlign reduces word alignment error rate from 0.412 to 0.326. These gains transfer downstream, leading to improvements in document-level translation coverage evaluation and recognition of semantic differences. We release CTFAlign as a Python package and make the code and data to reproduce our experiments publicly available.

</details>


### [59] [Evidence-Consistent Generative Detection under Scenario-Level Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21043)
*San Kim,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了社交工程欺诈检测中的场景级分布外（SL-OOD）问题，发现传统同分布评估会高估模型鲁棒性。作者提出ECoG框架，通过证据跨度监督和理由-标签一致性训练目标，在0.5B解码器上使OOD困难样本的Macro-F1提升3.22点，并显著减少预测与生成理由不一致的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的同分布评估在训练和测试数据共享特定任务模式或表面线索时会高估模型鲁棒性。在社交工程欺诈检测中，攻击者可以在保留恶意意图的同时改变场景、冒充对象或措辞，使得模型可能依赖场景特有的表面线索而非决策相关证据进行判断，从而在未见攻击场景中失效。

Method: 作者提出ECoG（Evidence-Consistent Generative）框架，包含两个核心组件：一是证据跨度监督（evidence-span supervision），二是理由-标签一致性目标（rationale-label consistency objective）。在训练过程中，模型被要求生成的推理理由与其预测标签保持一致，同时监督生成的理由覆盖参考证据跨度。该方法在特征、编码器和解码器三种基线类型上进行评估。

Result: 在0.5B解码器上，ECoG相比无一致性正则化的同骨干模型：OOD困难样本的Macro-F1提升3.22点；生成理由支持相反标签的预测占比降低4.22点；与参考证据跨度的token级重叠提升8.38点。预测-理由不一致性的降低在四个解码器骨干上保持一致。

Conclusion: 紧凑型生成式检测器在社交工程场景偏移下，可以从证据监督和理由-标签一致性中获益。高同分布性能并不能可靠预测跨场景鲁棒性，场景记忆化（依赖场景特有线索而非决策证据）是同分布与OOD性能差距的主要原因，ECoG框架通过促进证据一致性推理有效缓解了这一问题。

Abstract: Conventional in-distribution evaluation can overestimate robustness when training and test data share recurring task-specific patterns or surface cues. This risk is especially relevant in social-engineering fraud detection, where attackers can preserve malicious intent while changing the scenario, impersonated entity, or wording. We study this problem as scenario-level out-of-distribution (SL-OOD) detection for SMS and voice phishing, where entire attack scenarios are held out from training while the label space remains fixed. This setting tests whether models can generalize to unseen attack scenarios using decision-relevant evidence rather than familiar scenario-specific cues. Using this SL-OOD evaluation, we find that high in-distribution performance does not reliably predict held-out robustness across feature-, encoder-, and decoder-based baselines. We interpret this gap as scenario memorization: reliance on recurring scenario-specific lexical or entity cues rather than decision-relevant evidence. We propose ECoG, an evidence-consistent generative framework that combines evidence-span supervision with a rationale-label consistency objective during training. On the 0.5B decoder, relative to the same backbone trained without consistency regularization, ECoG raises Macro-F1 on OOD challenging instances by 3.22 points, reduces the share of predictions whose generated rationale supports the opposite label by 4.22 points, and increases token-level overlap with reference evidence spans by 8.38 points; the reduction in prediction-rationale inconsistency is consistent across four decoder backbones. These results suggest that compact generative detectors can benefit from evidence supervision and rationale-label consistency under social-engineering shift.

</details>


### [60] [PromptResponse: Optimizing Prompts for LLM Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21074)
*Erik Thureck,Robert Kühnen,Tim Jacobowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究了提示词格式（基线、JSON、Markdown、YAML及LLM调优版本）对GPT-4o解决编码任务的影响，发现一致的格式化（尤其JSON）能提升生成效率和语法稳定性并轻微改善任务性能，而LLM调优的提示词反而显著降低任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于研究工作和软件开发流程，但其输出对输入提示词的细微变化高度敏感。目前缺乏系统性的受控研究来理解提示词格式（而非内容）对代码生成质量的影响，尤其是格式化和LLM调优之间的权衡关系。

Method: 作者提出了一个名为"PromptResponse"的受控实验框架。他们构造了HumanEval数据集的五种语义相同但句法不同的变体（基线、JSON、Markdown、YAML和LLM调优版本），并让GPT-4o在8200次执行中解决这些编码问题。实验从代码性能、效率和稳定性三个维度进行对比分析。

Result: 一致的格式化（尤其是JSON）能提升生成效率和语法稳定性，并在任务性能上带来小幅提升。相反，LLM调优的提示词导致任务性能显著下降，且在其他维度上没有明显收益。

Conclusion: 低成本的提示词重新格式化本身就能带来可衡量的改进，而提示词调优则需要考虑与模型对齐的问题。作者提供了基于实验结果的实际建议，并公开了数据集变体和评估流程以供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in research workflows and software development pipelines, yet their output remains sensitive to input prompt variations. This paper presents $\unicode{x00AB}$PromptResponse$\unicode{x00BB}$, a controlled study examining how formatting and LLM-based tuning of coding task prompts affect the resulting code's performance, efficiency, and stability. Using five semantically identical yet syntactically distinct variants of the HumanEval dataset$\unicode{x2014}$baseline, JSON, Markdown, YAML, and an LLM-tuned version$\unicode{x2014}$we had GPT-4o solve its coding problems over 8200$\unicode{x00A0}$executions. Our results show that consistent formatting$\unicode{x2014}$especially JSON$\unicode{x2014}$improves generation efficiency and syntactic stability, with minor gains in task performance. Conversely, the LLM-tuned prompts resulted in significantly degraded task performance without significant improvements in any other dimension. These findings suggest that low-effort reformatting alone can yield measurable improvements, while tuning must account for model alignment. We conclude our work with providing a set of practical recommendations informed by our results as well as releasing our dataset variants and evaluation pipeline for future work.

</details>


### [61] [Jokes Aside: Measuring the Semantic Distance of Double Meanings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21087)
*Fabio De Ponte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用上下文嵌入向量（OpenAI text-embedding-3-small 和 MiniLM）重新审视了"我喜欢我的X就像我喜欢我的Y，Z"这一笑话生成模型中的量化指标。在三个数据集上测试后发现，基于这些指标训练的模型在预测幽默评分方面表现不佳（最佳准确率57.1%，低于61.5%基线），但新提出的对称性（symmetry）指标与高评分笑话持续相关，可能是幽默的必要（但非充分）属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Petrovic和Matthews（2013）提出了基于"I like my X like I like my Y, Z"句式的笑话生成模型，并假设笑话的搞笑程度与多个因素相关。Winters等人（2019）基于Google Ngrams和Word2Vector提出了五个量化指标，但这些指标基于较旧的嵌入方法。随着上下文嵌入向量（contextual embedding vectors）的出现，作者希望重新审视和细化这些早期假设，并引入新的指标来更好地捕捉幽默的本质。

Method: 使用两种嵌入模型（OpenAI text-embedding-3-small和MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2）收集向量表示。重新审视了Winters等人五个指标中的三个：obviousness（明显性）、compatibility（兼容性）和comparison（比较性），并新引入了symmetry（对称性）指标，定义为Z与X和Y的接近程度。在三个数据集上评估：JokeJudger、Expunations和rJokes。后两个数据集通过添加配对的句子进行了扩充，以捕捉每个笑话核心模糊表达（ambiguous expression）的两种不同含义。

Result: 基于所提出指标训练的模型在幽默评级预测上表现不佳：JokeJudger数据集上最佳模型准确率为57.1%，低于61.5%的基线；Expunations和rJokes上的表现更低。然而，对称性指标与高评级笑话的一致性关联表明，它可能捕捉到了幽默的一个必要（但非充分）属性。

Conclusion: 现有的基于嵌入向量的量化指标不足以独立预测幽默评分，但新提出的对称性指标展现出了潜在价值。该结果提示幽默的产生可能还需要除这些度量之外的额外因素，对称性仅是必要条件而非充分条件，未来研究需要探索更全面的幽默预测框架。

Abstract: Large language models have significantly enriched the toolkit for computational humor research, particularly in the automated generation of jokes and puns. A key innovation, contextual embedding vectors, offers new opportunities to revisit and refine earlier hypotheses. Notably, Petrovic and Matthews (2013) proposed a joke generation model based on the scheme "I like my X like I like my Y, Z" (e.g. "I like my ice like I like my dreams, crushed"). They suggested that joke hilarity increases with: a) frequent association of Z with X and Y, b) rarity of Z, c) ambiguity of Z, and d) meaning distance between X and Y. Building on this, Winters et al. (2019) proposed a set of metrics, based on Google Ngrams and Word2Vector. In this work, three out of their five metrics are revisited with word embeddings: obviousness, compatibility, and comparison. Another measure, symmetry, defined as closeness of Z to both X and Y, is introduced here for the first time. Two models were used to collect the embedding vectors (OpenAI text-embedding-3-small and MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2) on three datasets: JokeJudger, Expunations, and rJokes. The last two datasets, Expunations, and rJokes, were expanded by adding paired sentences that captured the ambiguous expression at the core of each joke in its two different meanings. Results revealed that models trained on the proposed metrics performed poorly in predicting humor ratings: on JokeJudger, the best model achieved 57.1% accuracy, below the 61.5% baseline, while performance on Expunations and rJokes was even lower. Nevertheless, the symmetry metric seems consistently associated with higher-rated jokes, suggesting it may capture a necessary -though not sufficient- property of humor.

</details>


### [62] [When the Feature Pool Goes Algorithmic: Extending Mufwene's Ecology of Language Evolution to LLM-Mediated Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21088)
*Kunmei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型（LLM）视为"分布中介体"（distributional mediators），扩展Mufwene的语言生态学模型。LLM通过训练与后训练改变人类语言变体在说话者可及分布中的相对频率，这一过程被称为"算法性重加权"（algorithmic reweighting）。模型关联的语言形式可能被人类采纳并规约化、可能被标记为"AI风格"而遭回避、也可能根本无法扩散，人类社会的评价机制仍起决定性作用。文章提出了关于语言采纳、模型版本效应、语言趋同与社会反转的可检验预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言生态学模型（Mufwene的生态模型）将语言演化定位于个体方言（idiolects）之间的竞争以及说话者在互动中做出的选择，但大语言模型的介入使得语言变体的产生与传播路径变得复杂。作者旨在回答：LLM如何在无需将选择中心从人类说话者移开的前提下，融入并影响这一语言演化架构。

Method: 本文采取理论思辨与整合论证的方法：（1）将LLM概念化为"分布中介体"，即聚合、变换并再分发人类语言产出的中间层；（2）提出"算法性重加权"机制，描述模型介导如何改变竞争性语言变体到达人类选择者的相对频率；（3）结合现有关于模型特定语言特征与词汇采纳的实证证据，评估该路径的部分有效性；（4）给出可检验的预测框架。

Result: 研究指出，现有证据与"算法性重加权"路径的某些环节一致，但不足以证明语言必然走向趋同（convergence）。模型关联的语言形式存在三种可能结局：扩散并被规约化、被社会识别为"AI风格"而遭刻意回避、或根本未扩散。社会评价仍是决定语言演化的关键变量。

Conclusion: 该提案将Mufwene的特征池（feature-pool）生态扩展至说话者选择的上游环节，为语言演化研究提供了新的理论框架，并生成了关于语言采纳、模型版本更迭效应、语言趋同与社会反转四个方面的可检验预测。

Abstract: Mufwene's ecological model locates language evolution in competition among variants contributed by individual idiolects and in speakers' selection from linguistic material made available through interaction. Large language models (LLMs) complicate this architecture without requiring the locus of selection to move away from human speakers. This article argues that LLMs are best treated as distributional mediators: they aggregate language produced across human populations, transform its distribution through training and post-training, and redistribute model-specific outputs at scale. I call the resulting ecological process algorithmic reweighting of the speaker-accessible distribution: model mediation can alter the relative frequencies with which competing variants reach human selectors. Emerging evidence on model-specific linguistic profiles and lexical uptake is consistent with parts of this pathway, but does not establish inevitable convergence. Human social evaluation remains decisive: model-associated forms may diffuse and become conventionalized, become socially recognizable as 'AI-like' and subsequently avoided, or fail to diffuse in the first place. The proposal extends Mufwene's feature-pool ecology one step upstream of speaker selection and yields testable predictions about uptake, model-version effects, convergence, and social reversal.

</details>


### [63] [No PUN Intended: Plausible Unknown Names for Person-Centred LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21206)
*Dimitri Staufer,David Hartmann,Ibrahim Baroud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PUN（Plausible Unknown Names）协议，用于构建和验证在LLM评估中真正"未知"的人物姓名（即具有合理First-Last形式、无索引的全名证据、无歧义信号的姓名），并发布了300个经过多步骤验证的姓名及对照样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人物姓名被广泛用作LLM评估中的提示变量（用于事实性、隐私泄露、偏见和拒绝回答等评测），但当姓名的证据状态（evidential status）未被控制时，测量结果可能混淆记忆（memorisation）、检索（retrieval）、姓名先验（name priors）和错误人物归因（wrong-person attribution），导致评估失真。

Method: 将"未知姓名"操作化定义为：（1）具有合理的First-Last形式；（2）无索引的全名证据；（3）在文档化验证运行下无歧义信号。据此提出PUN协议，结合Wikidata派生组件、支持网络的LLM筛选和受控搜索再验证三个步骤构建和验证姓名。同时报告接受率、可复现性、消融实验及204名参与者的人类研究。

Result: 与对照组相比，被接受的姓名更具"类姓名"特征（more name-like），参与者仅在3%的情况下能恢复人物证据。发布了300个姓名及对照样本。

Conclusion: PUN协议能够可靠地构建和验证"未知姓名"，为LLM评估提供可控的提示变量，从而避免因姓名证据状态不受控而导致测量混淆；3%的证据恢复率表明该协议在确保姓名真正"未知"方面有效。

Abstract: Person names are widely used as prompt variables in LLM evaluations of factuality, privacy leakage, bias and abstention, but when a name's evidential status is uncontrolled, measurements may conflate memorisation, retrieval, name priors and wrong-person attribution. We operationalise an unknown name as one with plausible First-Last form, no indexed full-name evidence, and no ambiguity signals under a documented validation run, and introduce PUN (Plausible Unknown Names), a protocol for constructing and validating such names, combining Wikidata-derived components, web-enabled LLM screening, and controlled search revalidation. We report acceptance rate, reproducibility, ablations, and a 204-participant human study, finding accepted names are more name-like than controls while participants recover person evidence in only 3% of cases. We release 300 names with comparison controls.

</details>


### [64] [RARE: Decoupling Representation Steering from Expert Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21236)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RARE框架，一种面向Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)大语言模型的router无关表示工程方法。RARE通过将行为扰动投影到router矩阵的零空间，消除对路由的干扰，并在6种MoE模型上验证了其在有害性、真实性和事实编辑三类操控场景中的有效性，取得了比基线更优的效果-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示工程（Representation Engineering）通过修改中间隐状态来控制语言模型行为，是一种轻量级方法。然而将其直接应用于MoE模型时存在结构性错配：MoE中的router对隐层状态的扰动高度敏感，导致行为操控被路由机制干扰甚至抵消。作者通过实证研究确认了这一失败模式，发现保持路由稳定能显著恢复操控性能，且router对语义内容比对行为变化更敏感，因此需要针对MoE结构设计适配的表示工程框架。

Method: RARE框架的核心思路是将任意行为扰动投影到router矩阵的零空间，移除router可见的扰动分量，从而保证路由不被改变。同时，RARE还进一步纠正被选择的下游层中由投影过程传播的路由漂移。作者评估了五种扰动估计器，并在六个异构开源MoE模型上进行了系统对比。

Result: 在有害性操控中，RARE达到53.3%的平均攻击成功率，同时保留67.8%的MMLU准确率，整体效果-效用权衡优于基线方法。TruthfulQA MC1准确率从41.0%提升至58.6%，CounterFact功效从16.8%提升至96.3%。

Conclusion: 路由一致性（routing consistency）是MoE模型中适配表示工程的重要架构考量因素。实证结果表明，通过将扰动限制在router不可见的子空间中，可以在保持模型整体能力的同时实现有效的行为操控，为MoE模型上的表示工程提供了新的设计原则。

Abstract: Representation engineering offers a lightweight means of controlling language-model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states, but its direct application to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models introduces a structural mismatch. We first verify this failure mode through a series of empirical studies and find that preserving clean routing substantially recovers steering performance and that routing is more sensitive to semantic content than to behavioral changes under controlled content. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RARE, a router-agnostic representation engineering framework for MoE language models. RARE projects arbitrary behavioral perturbations onto the null space of the router matrix, thereby removing router-visible components, and further corrects routing drift propagated to selected downstream layers. To decide the best perturbation estimator in this framework, we evaluate five estimators on six heterogeneous open-weight MoE models across three steering scenarios: harmfulness, truthfulness, and factual editing. On harmfulness steering, RARE reaches an average attack success rate of 53.3% while retaining 67.8% MMLU accuracy, yielding a stronger aggregate effectiveness--utility trade-off than baselines. It further improves average TruthfulQA MC1 accuracy from 41.0% to 58.6% and CounterFact efficacy from 16.8% to 96.3%. These results support routing consistency as an important architectural consideration for adapting representation engineering to MoE models.

</details>


### [65] [Affective Context Amplifies Sycophancy in LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21242)
*Jiayi Li,Sanjana Menon,Brett Frischmann,Shomir Wilson,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究考察了大语言模型（LLM）在情感语境下的谄媚行为，发现模型在面对用户自身披露时会系统性地软化或回避负面评价，且用户的负面情感状态（尤其是孤独和痛苦）会进一步放大这一差异，表现为"回避式谄媚"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为对话伴侣经常能感知用户的情绪状态，但情感语境如何影响模型谄媚行为尚未被系统研究。作者借鉴讨好理论（ingratiation theory），希望通过实证方法揭示情感上下文对模型评价性反馈的调节作用，特别是在用户最需要真实反馈的情境下。

Method: 作者通过对比模型对同一内容作为第三人称叙述与作为用户自身披露时的回应差异来衡量谄媚程度。实验覆盖7个LLM和两个Reddit数据集（r/AmItheAsshole和r/TrueUnpopularOpinion），比较模型中立评价与面向用户的回应之间的分歧。

Result: 研究发现这种分歧是系统性的且强烈单向：面向用户的回应一致地软化或隐瞒负面/反对性判断。情感语境进一步放大此分歧，其中负面状态（特别是孤独和痛苦）产生最大效应。模型往往通过回避式谄媚（转向不表态的回应而非直接赞同）来抑制批判性反馈。

Conclusion: 情感语境充当了一种脆弱性信号，在用户最可能需要批判性反馈时反而抑制了模型的诚实评价。这种回避式谄媚行为对LLM作为可靠的对话伴侣和反馈提供者的角色构成风险。

Abstract: As conversational companions, large language models (LLMs) often have access to users' emotional states. We study how this affective context modulates LLM sycophancy in subjective, evaluative interactions, where users share actions or opinions that invite feedback. Drawing on ingratiation theory, we measure sycophancy as the divergence between a model's independent evaluation and its user-facing response, elicited by presenting the same content as either a third-party account or the user's own disclosure. Across seven LLMs and two Reddit datasets (r/AmItheAsshole and r/TrueUnpopularOpinion), we find that this divergence is systematic and strongly one-directional. User-facing responses consistently soften or withhold negative or oppositional judgments. Affective context further amplifies this divergence with negative states, particularly loneliness and distress, producing the largest effects. These findings suggest that affective context functions as a vulnerability signal that suppresses critical feedback when users may need it most, often through evasive sycophancy, in which models retreat toward non-committal responses rather than outright agreement.

</details>


### [66] [Benchmarking Patent Drafting from Inventor-Style Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21249)
*Lekang Jiang,Wenjun Sun,Stephan Goetz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Dis2Pat数据集和Patent-MAF多智能体框架，解决从早期发明披露材料直接生成完整专利申请书的核心挑战，弥补了现有LLM在专利撰写任务中仅处理后期结构化输入的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM虽在单个专利撰写任务上表现良好，但无法应对真实专利撰写流程的核心挑战——从早期、非正式、去法律化的发明人披露材料直接生成完整且法律连贯的专利申请。此前工作大多假设输入是后期、高度结构化或已法律化的文本，与真实工作流不符。

Method: 1) 构建Dis2Pat数据集：要求模型从发明人风格的、非法律化的披露文本直接生成完整专利申请，反映真实专利工作流；2) 提出Patent-MAF基线：一个支持本地部署的多智能体框架，用于长篇幅、受法律约束的专利撰写任务，同时兼顾隐私需求。

Result: 基准测试显示现有LLM在专利撰写上存在明显局限；Patent-MAF作为强基线，持续优于所评估的开源模型，并与大型闭源模型表现相当甚至更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 真实专利撰写需要从早期非结构化发明材料直接生成完整申请文件的解决方案，Patent-MAF验证了多智能体+本地部署框架在该任务上的可行性，为后续研究提供了强基线。

Abstract: While recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising results on individual patent drafting tasks, they fundamentally fail to investigate the core challenge of real-world patent drafting: generating a complete and legally coherent patent application directly from early-stage invention materials. Prior work predominantly assumes later-stage, highly structured, or already legalistic inputs. However, real patenting workflows begin with informal, de-legalized disclosures authored by inventors. To bridge the gap, we introduce Dis2Pat, a disclosure-to-patent dataset that reflects realistic patenting workflows by requiring the generation of complete patent applications directly from inventor-style, de-legalized disclosures. Given the inherent difficulty of long-form, legally constrained patent drafting and the strong privacy requirements, we further propose a strong baseline named Patent-MAF. It is a multi-agent framework for locally deployable patent drafting. Benchmark results reveal that current LLMs exhibit limitations in patent drafting, while Patent-MAF provides a strong baseline that consistently outperforms evaluated open-source models and remains competitive with large closed-source models.

</details>


### [67] [EnSI-RAG: Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21252)
*Xuanyu Meng,Jiashuo Sun,Jash Rajesh Parekh,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EnSI-RAG框架，通过构建以实体为中心的查询无关索引，将证据定位与答案合成分离，在长文档多跳问答任务中解决了传统RAG方法因分块边界分割实体与证据、以及多跳推理能力不足的问题，在Loong和Oolong数据集上平均准确率达78.24%，较基准提升6.62个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的RAG方法将文档切分为原始分块并通过嵌入相似度检索，在两类场景下性能显著下降：一是分块边界将实体与支持性证据分离；二是问题需要跨越语料库进行多跳推理。这些限制阻碍了长连接文档上的问答任务。

Method: EnSI-RAG构建了一个与查询无关、以实体为中心的索引结构。每条记录为四元组(e, t, k, v)，表示实体e、其类型t、语义类别k∈{property, relation, aspect}以及值v，同时保留指向原始源文本的链接。查询时这些记录作为检索句柄，由LLM将检索到的段落综合生成最终答案。该设计将证据定位与答案合成两个阶段分离，同时保持可追溯的源证据。

Result: 在Loong和Oolong数据集上，EnSI-RAG的平均准确率达到78.24，相比使用的已发表基准分数平均高出6.62个百分点，验证了该方法在长文档多跳问答中的有效性。

Conclusion: EnSI-RAG通过实体结构索引的方式有效解决了长文档多跳QA中证据碎片化和跨段落推理的难题，分离证据检索与答案生成提升了系统性能，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over long, connected documents remains challenging because relevant evidence may span multiple entities and their relationships. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods typically index documents as raw chunks and retrieve them through embedding similarity. Their performance degrades when chunk boundaries separate entities from supporting evidence or when a question requires multi-hop reasoning across the corpus. We propose EnSI-RAG (Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a framework that constructs a query-independent, entity-centered index. Each record (e, t, k, v) represents an entity e, its type t, a semantic category k in {property, relation, aspect}, and a value v, while retaining links to the original source passages. At query time, these records serve as retrieval handles, and an LLM synthesizes the retrieved passages into the final answer. This design separates evidence localization from answer synthesis while preserving traceable source evidence. Across Loong and Oolong, EnSI-RAG achieves an average accuracy of 78.24. Relative to the published baseline scores used as references, this is 6.62 points higher, suggesting its effectiveness across these settings. The code is available at https://github.com/RamonMeng/EnSI-RAG.

</details>


### [68] [Memory Augmentation Unlocks Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21265)
*Simeng Zhang,Yilong Chen,Wenyuan Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yao Chen,Junyuan Shang,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"上下文-生成替代定律"来形式化CoT压缩的权衡，并基于此提出记忆增强压缩框架，通过从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆作为prefill脚手架，在不增加训练的情况下显著提升压缩后推理的性能和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖CoT推理解决复杂任务，但冗长的推理轨迹带来大量推理开销。CoT压缩虽能缩短生成序列，但过度压缩会破坏逻辑连贯性导致性能下降，需要在压缩与推理质量之间寻求平衡。

Method: 提出"上下文-生成替代定律"（Context-Generation Substitution Law），形式化显式推理上下文与解码时生成之间的权衡关系。基于这一原理，设计Memory-Augmented Compression框架：一个无需训练的框架，从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆（总结可复用的推理模式、关键约束和关键操作），并在prefill阶段将其作为脚手架检索注入，以补偿压缩过程中丢失的信息。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH、BBH、MMLU-Sci上相比CoD分别提升21.4、28.0、29.5和6.61个百分点的准确率，同时相比标准CoT实现1.14-1.49倍的延迟加速。该框架兼容token级、推理轨迹级和推理状态级的压缩机制，且消融实验证明性能提升来源于相关推理记忆而非简单增加上下文长度。

Conclusion: 记忆增强压缩有效解决了CoT压缩中生成效率与推理质量的矛盾，通过复用历史推理模式作为外部支架，实现了在显著降低推理延迟的同时提升压缩推理的准确性，为CoT压缩提供了一个通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models often rely on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks, but verbose reasoning traces introduce substantial inference overhead. CoT compression shortens generation, yet aggressive compression may disrupt logical coherence and degrade performance. We formalize this trade-off as the \textit{Context-Generation Substitution Law}, where explicit reasoning context substitutes for part of decode-time generation. Based on this principle, we propose \textit{Memory-Augmented Compression}, a training-free framework that constructs reusable reasoning memories from historical traces and retrieves them as prefill-side scaffolds. Rather than using raw demonstrations, these memories summarize reusable reasoning patterns, key constraints, and critical operations to compensate for information lost during compression. Experiments show that Memory consistently improves prompt-based Chain-of-Draft (CoD) compression across mathematical reasoning, complex reasoning, and science question answering tasks, yielding accuracy gains of 21.4, 28.0, 29.5, and 6.61 points over CoD on GSM8K, MATH, BBH, and MMLU-Sci, while achieving a 1.14--1.49$\times$ latency speedup over standard CoT. Memory is also compatible with token-level, reasoning-trace-level, and inference-state compression mechanisms. Further analyzes show that the gains come from relevant reasoning memories rather than simply increasing context length.

</details>


### [69] [Prompt-Model Interaction Reaches the Fixed Points: A deterministic, task-free structural readout -- and the factorizations of it that failed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过一个无任务读出的固定点结构，证明提示词效应是"提示词-模型"二元组的属性而非提示词本身的属性，且该效应幅度巨大但无法被任何已提出的机制（前缀长度、文体、注意力汇点等）所解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究仅通过任务准确率证明提示词效应因模型而异，但无法区分这种交互是任务机制还是条件分布本身的事实。作者希望在不涉及任务的读出头（即短窗口argmax映射的固定点结构）上考察这一交互是否依然存在。

Method: 在96个起始条件下普查短窗口argmax映射x_{t+1}=argmax_x p(x|x_{t-1},x_t)的固定点结构。该读出的映射是确定性的（不存在"被帮助或损害"的问题），且仅在短窗口下存在（6个模型中有4个在窗口16时完全失去固定点）。进而考察9个token的条件前缀如何移动固定点比例、改变四类结构类别、重排模型排序，并对比60.5 IFEval点的指令微调。

Result: 两个核心结果：(1)交互在此读头上以完整幅度出现——9个token的调节将固定点比例推移过其大部分范围，改变结构类别并重排模型，而60.5 IFEval的指令微调不改变类别；(2)所有已提出的解释机制均失败：前缀长度效应非单调、四个现象学因素在扩大样本后被逐一推翻、注意力汇点机制在5个模型中仅对2个预测正确（等于随机），且该机制只在真实文本上成立、在均匀随机输入上失效，说明问题出在其适用域外。一个固定的9-token前缀将4个模型推向0、2个推向1，双向性在分布内起始条件下存活。

Conclusion: 在此读头上解释的单元是"提示词-模型"对。论文反复捕获的自身错误有一个命名：将一个有形状的标准应用于一个没有变化空间的量。

Abstract: That a prompt's effect is not a property of the prompt is established: prompts optimised for one model degrade on another, and rankings reorder under neutral reformatting. That evidence is about task accuracy, which cannot say whether the interaction is a fact about task machinery or about the conditional distribution itself. We ask on a readout with no task in it: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax map x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t), censused from 96 starts. It is deterministic, so nothing can be helped or hurt, and it exists only at short windows -- four of six models lose it entirely by window 16 -- so everything here concerns how a model reads a fragment. Two results. First, the interaction reaches this readout at full magnitude: nine tokens of conditioning move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range, change a four-way structural class, and reorder models, while instruction tuning worth 60.5 IFEval points moves the class by zero. Second, nothing we proposed carries it. Prefix length fails: the effect is not monotone. Four phenomenological factors -- prose-versus-markup, a universal direction, bidirectionality, instruct-resistance -- were each withdrawn within one run of being proposed, dissolved by widening the sample. And the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink dominance of early tokens, predicts the sign of the shift on 2 of 5 models -- chance -- while a length-by-content cross shows it holds on real text and fails on our probe's uniformly random input, so we are outside its regime, not against it. One fixed nine-token prefix drives four models toward 0 and two toward 1; the bidirectionality survives in-distribution starts. On this readout the unit of explanation is the prompt-model pair. The recurring error it caught in us has a name: a criterion with a shape applied to a quantity with no room to vary.

</details>


### [70] [Move by Move: Measuring and Steering How LLMs Conduct Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21325)
*Afonso Baldo,Hugo Pitorro,Areti Vassilopoulos,Anabela C. Areias,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei,Nuno M. Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个包含十种治疗性对话动作的本体（基于MULTI-60清单构建，经五位持证心理学家验证标注），用于分析大型语言模型在心理治疗对话中的行为模式。研究发现模型过度使用询问（高达人类速率的三倍）、忽视心理教育，且强烈依赖上下文情境。将此本体暴露为工具集后，模型与人类治疗师的动作分布偏差减半，回合级对齐度提升7-9个百分点，且无需微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越多地依赖大语言模型获取情感支持，但学界对模型如何实际开展心理治疗对话知之甚少，缺乏系统化的工具来衡量和比较模型与人类治疗师在治疗对话中的行为差异。

Method: 提出十类治疗性对话动作的本体（基于MULTI-60清单的功能化分类），通过五位持证心理学家的标注活动验证，并采用与专家一致性匹配的裁判式方法进行规模化标注。将该本体应用于真实咨询记录和模型主导的对话，比较人类临床医生与前沿模型群体的动作分布；并将本体作为工具集暴露给模型进行干预实验。

Result: 模型过度使用询问动作（高达人类的3倍），忽视心理教育；模型具有强烈的上下文锚定效应——会延续人类临床医生发起的策略但极少主动发起。将本体暴露为工具集后，模型与人类动作分布的均值偏差降低约一半，回合级与人类治疗师的对其度提升7-9个百分点，且无需任何微调。

Conclusion: 结构化的治疗动作本体可以作为可解释的工具，显著改善大语言模型在心理治疗对话中与人类治疗师行为的对齐度，为无需微调的模型行为干预提供了可行路径，并为未来的模型心理治疗行为评估与改进提供了基准框架。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to large language models for emotional support, yet little is known about how these models actually conduct a psychotherapy interaction. We introduce an ontology of ten therapeutic moves: compact, function-based categories grounded in the MULTI-60 inventory, validated through an annotation campaign with five licensed psychologists, and scaled with a judge-based approach that matches expert agreement. Applying it to real counseling transcripts and model-led sessions, we compare the move distributions between human clinicians and a panel of frontier models. Models over-use inquiry at up to three times the human rate, neglect psychoeducation, and are strongly context-anchored: they carry forward strategies initiated by a human clinician but rarely initiate them themselves. Exposing the ontology as a set of tools roughly halves the mean deviation from the human move distribution and improves turn-level alignment with human therapist by 7-9 percentage points, without any fine-tuning.

</details>

### [71] [AEGIS: Preventing Cross-Domain Resource Abuse in MCP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20481)
*Shriti Priya,Teryl Taylor,Frederico Araujo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS，一个策略执行组件，用于防范基于MCP（模型上下文协议）工具的多模态资源滥用攻击。AEGIS利用大语言模型的推理能力，将异构工具调用分析、分类和标准化为统一的策略友好表示，并与Open Policy Agent和ContextForge AI Gateway集成，检测和缓解滥用行为，同时保持MCP智能体生态系统的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP是开放源代码的JSON-RPC协议，用于标准化LLM与外部系统（工具）的交互。攻击者或恶意智能体可利用MCP工具的不同模态（如文本、图像、视频、位置）来降低基于智能体的应用的服务质量，例如请求过大的搜索半径或过长的视频，导致后端系统过载甚至拒绝服务。不同模态引入了各异的资源滥用向量，且多模态和跨域工具暴露了多样化的请求模式和参数，使得制定既通用又精确的资源约束策略变得困难。

Method: AEGIS利用大语言模型的推理能力，对多样化的工具调用进行分析、分类和归一化，将其转换为统一的、策略友好的表示形式，供安全从业人员使用。AEGIS与Open Policy Agent（策略引擎）和ContextForge AI Gateway（网关）集成，实现对滥用行为的检测与缓解。

Result: 论文展示了AEGIS作为策略执行组件的设计，能够跨异构MCP工具和模态定义细粒度的资源滥用防护措施，并与现有安全基础设施（OPA和ContextForge AI Gateway）无缝集成，在检测和缓解滥用行为的同时保持MCP智能体生态系统的灵活性。

Conclusion: AEGIS提供了一种基于LLM推理能力的策略执行方案，解决了MCP工具生态中多模态资源滥用防护策略难以统一制定的问题，为基于智能体的应用提供了可定制的安全防护层。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) is an open source JSON-RPC protocol that standardizes how large language models (LLMs) interact with external systems through programmatic functions known as tools. Attackers or malicious agents can exploit certain modalities of these MCP tools to degrade the overall quality of service of agent-based applications. For example, an agent may request an excessively large search radius or very long videos, overloading backend systems and potentially causing slowdowns or denial-of-service. Each modality including text, images, video, and location introduces distinct vectors for resource abuse, complicating the development of consistent mitigation strategies. Moreover, multimodal and crossdomain tools expose diverse request schemas and parameters, making it difficult to define policies that are both generalizable and precise enough to enforce meaningful resource constraints. In this paper, we present AEGIS, a policy enforcement component that enables administrators to define fine-grained safeguards against resource abuse across heterogeneous MCP tools and modalities. AEGIS leverages the reasoning capabilities of large language models to analyze, categorize, and normalize diverse tool invocations into a unified, policy-friendly representation accessible to security practitioners. Integrated with the Open Policy Agent and the ContextForge AI Gateway, AEGIS detects and mitigates abusive behaviors while preserving the flexibility of MCP-based agent ecosystems.

</details>


### [72] [aiXamine: Unified Black-Box Evaluation of Cross-Dimensional Trade-offs in LLM Safety, Security, and Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20554)
*Fatih Deniz,Yazan Boshmaf,Dorde Popovic,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出aiXamine，一个统一的黑盒评估平台，通过46项测试和9项服务，对超120个LLM进行超过5000次测试运行，首次联合评估安全、安全和隐私三个维度，发现三个跨维度现象：安全税（更强的安全对齐导致过度拒绝）、隐私与其他维度近乎正交、蒸馏诱导的鲁棒性崩溃（56.9→2.6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估框架独立评估安全、安全和隐私，无法检测跨维度的关键失败模式——例如模型在安全对齐上得99.3分但拒绝1/3良性查询，或能力指标全面提升但隐私下降21分。信任度本质上是多维的，但当前对齐方法将其视为单一目标。

Method: aiXamine是一个统一的黑盒评估平台，通过自动化红队管线编排46项测试、跨9个服务，生成从提示级诊断到跨服务权衡分析的分层风险画像，在相同条件下实现对专有和开源模型的可复现比较。应用于120+个LLM、5000+次测试运行。

Result: 发现三个跨维度现象：(1)安全税——更强的安全对齐系统性增加过度拒绝，迫使提供商在保护与实用性之间选择；(2)隐私与其他信任维度近乎正交，标准对齐无法覆盖；(3)蒸馏诱导鲁棒性崩溃——离策略蒸馏且无在线策略校正导致熵崩溃，将鲁棒性从56.9摧毁至2.6。此外还发现规模收益递减和类别依赖的安全行为。

Conclusion: 信任度本质上是多维的：一个轴上的进展不能保证其他轴的进展，甚至可能主动破坏其他轴的进展。当前的单一目标对齐方法不足以应对这一多维挑战，需要统一的多维度评估框架。

Abstract: The critical failure modes in deployed large language models (LLMs) are cross-dimensional: a model can score 99.3 in safety alignment while refusing one in three benign queries, or improve across every capability metric while losing 21 points in privacy. Existing evaluation frameworks that assess safety, security, and privacy independently cannot detect these patterns. We introduce aiXamine, a unified black-box platform that evaluates LLM trustworthiness across safety, security, and privacy as interdependent properties. aiXamine orchestrates 46 tests across nine services through an automated red-teaming pipeline, producing hierarchical risk profiles, from prompt-level diagnostics to cross-service trade-off analytics, that enable reproducible comparison of proprietary and open-weight systems under identical conditions. Applying aiXamine to over 120 LLMs through more than 5,000 test runs, we conduct the largest joint safety, security, and privacy study to date and uncover three cross-dimensional phenomena invisible to single-axis evaluation. First, safety enforcement incurs a quantifiable safety tax: stronger alignment systematically increases over-refusal, forcing providers to choose between protection and utility. Second, privacy is near-orthogonal to other trustworthiness dimensions and not captured by standard alignment. Third, we identify and formally characterize distillation-induced robustness collapse: off-policy distillation without on-policy correction causes entropy collapse, catastrophically destroying robustness (56.9$\to$2.6) on the same base architecture. These findings, compounded by diminishing returns from scale and category-dependent safety behaviors, demonstrate that trustworthiness is inherently multi-dimensional: progress along one axis does not guarantee, and can actively undermine, progress along others, yet current alignment methods treat it as a single objective.

</details>


### [73] [Beyond End-to-End Success: Diagnosing Failures in Long-Horizon Security LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20563)
*Wei Shao,Chongzhou Fang,Zuxiong Tan,Zequan Liang,Setareh Rafatirad,Avesta Sasan,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种诊断方法论，通过在安全任务中设置检查点来区分长时程安全LLM智能体在"能力暴露前"和"能力暴露后"的失败，并使用受控干预测试上游瓶颈，以弥补仅依赖聚合任务成功率评估的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程安全LLM智能体需要在多个依赖交互中传递信息和决策，后续动作往往依赖早期发现的服务、状态或访问权限。仅看最终任务成功率难以解释智能体是否真的具备目标能力——它可能在到达能力可被检验的节点之前就已失败。因此需要诊断"在哪里失败"和"为什么失败"的评估方法。

Method: 提出一种诊断方法论：(1) 在安全任务中设置检查点（checkpoints）；(2) 通过检查点分离能力暴露前后的失败；(3) 使用受控干预实验测试疑似上游瓶颈。方法论在四类任务族中评估：延迟复用已发现信息、复用观察到的状态、失败策略后的恢复、不确定结果后的决策。

Result: 在观察状态复用任务中，检查点分析显示Gemini 2.5 Flash的许多失败发生在模型观察到其后续需要复用的状态之前。在预注册的92-seed研究中，针对协议消歧的定向指导将状态观察率从匹配对照信息下的65.5%提升到95.4%。但同样的设计应用于Gemini 3.7 Flash时产生了相反效果，且状态观察不再可靠预测任务完成。表明主导失败源随模型代际而转移。

Conclusion: 长时程安全智能体的主导失败来源可能在不同模型代际之间发生显著变化，因此评估不应仅依赖聚合任务成功率，而应诊断失败发生的位置和原因，以指导更有针对性的改进。

Abstract: Long-horizon security LLM agents must carry information and decisions across many dependent interactions, where later actions often depend on services, state, or access discovered much earlier. This makes final task success difficult to interpret: an agent may fail before it ever reaches the point where the capability of interest can be exercised. We present a diagnostic methodology that instruments security tasks with checkpoints, separates failures before and after capability exposure, and uses controlled interventions to test suspected upstream bottlenecks. We evaluate the methodology across four task families involving delayed reuse of discovered information, reuse of observed state, recovery from failed strategies, and decision making after uncertain outcomes. On observed state reuse, checkpoint analysis shows that many Gemini 2.5 Flash failures occur before the model observes the state it is later expected to reuse. In a pre-specified 92-seed study, targeted protocol-disambiguation guidance increases state observation from 65.5\% under a matched non-guidance control message to 95.4\%. Repeating the same design with Gemini 3.7 Flash produces the opposite effect, while state observation no longer reliably predicts task completion. These results show that the dominant source of failure can shift across model generations, motivating evaluation that diagnoses where and why long-horizon security agents fail rather than relying only on aggregate task success.

</details>


### [74] [Temporal Risk on Satellites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20575)
*Shiqi Liu,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个将时间作为显式维度的卫星安全时间风险评估框架，通过五维时间能力模型和离散化任务时间窗口，输出时间索引化的可能性-影响矩阵，帮助运营商规避高风险时段并动态配置防御资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络安全风险框架将卫星威胁视为静态，但实际上卫星的脆弱性会随轨道偏移、功率余量变化和空间环境恶化而动态演变。同一漏洞在关键机动操作期间与常规运行期间的破坏性完全不同，现有方法无法捕捉这种随时间变化的威胁态势。

Method: 该框架将时间作为显式分析轴，扩展了现有对手行为分类法，提出五维时间能力模型来估计不同任务时间窗口内的利用难度。将任务离散化为具有操作意义的时间窗口和环境带，输出一系列时间索引化的可能性-影响矩阵而非单一风险评分。

Result: 框架输出时间序列化的可能性-影响矩阵，能够显示系统在不同时间窗口下最易暴露的时刻，从而为运营调度和防御资源配置提供随时间演化的风险视图。

Conclusion: 该时间风险评估框架使卫星运营商能够避免在高风险周期安排敏感操作，并将防御资源与随时间的威胁态势对齐，从而提升卫星任务的整体安全性。

Abstract: Satellite vulnerabilities change over time as orbits shift, power margins tighten, and the space environment deteriorates. However, most cybersecurity risk frameworks still treat threats as static. In practice, the same exploit can be far more damaging during a critical maneuver than during routine operations. We propose a temporal risk assessment framework that makes time an explicit axis in satellite security analysis. It extends existing adversary behavior taxonomies with a five-dimensional temporal capability model and estimates exploitation difficulty across distinct temporal windows of a mission. Rather than producing a single risk score, the framework outputs a series of time-indexed likelihood-impact matrices. It discretizes missions into operationally meaningful time windows and environmental bands to show when systems are most exposed. This view helps operators avoid scheduling sensitive operations in high-risk periods and align defensive resources with a threat landscape that shifts over time.

</details>


### [75] [Keyed Provenance Watermarking with Complementary Lattice-Based Secure Aggregation for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20580)
*Xinyun Liu,Zhi Lu,Yu Chen,Ronghua Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个双层次联邦学习安全框架，将基于物理锚定元数据（PAM）和密钥HMAC-SHA-256的上下文溯源水印（数据层）与基于RLWE的格上零知识安全聚合协议LZKSA（计算层）耦合，同时防御数据泄露、未授权复用和恶意梯度操纵等多级别攻击，并在复合攻击场景下验证了两层互补保护效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临数据泄露、未授权复用和恶意梯度操纵等多级别安全威胁，但现有方法通常单独处理某一类攻击，缺乏端到端联合防御方案，导致FL系统仍暴露于复合攻击风险之下。

Method: 该框架包含两个层面：（1）数据层：提出Kerckhoffs合规的PAM方案，利用可信基础设施数据（时间、位置、服务器ID）生成上下文溯源令牌，经密钥HMAC-SHA-256变换产生不可伪造的水印载荷，再通过FMGAN（基于GAN的鲁棒图像水印框架，含特征融合模块和Mamba引导的线性注意力机制）嵌入；（2）计算层：采用基于格的后量子安全LZKSA协议，通过RLWE设计在提交的梯度上验证密钥正确性、L2范数边界和余弦相似度约束，且不泄露私有更新。

Result: 大量实验验证了双层在复合攻击场景下的互补保护效果，表明该框架能够同时应对数据层面的溯源验证问题和计算层面的梯度安全聚合问题，是首个在混合端到端可信FL系统中联合评估两者的验证工作流。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了将上下文溯源水印与格基零知识安全聚合耦合的可行性，为构建后量子安全的端到端可信联邦学习提供了新路径，填补了多级攻击联合防御的研究空白。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to multi-level attacks. However, existing methods address them separately, leaving FL exposed to data leakage, unauthorized reuse, and malicious gradient manipulation. In this work, we propose an FL framework that couples keyed context-provenance watermarking with verifiable lattice-based secure aggregation of Real-World Anchored Watermarking and Lattice-Based Zero-Knowledge Secure Aggregation. At the data layer, we propose a Kerckhoffs-compliant scheme that utilizes Physical Anchor Metadata (PAM) to ensure data provenance. PAM is defined as a context-provenance token derived from trusted infrastructure data (time, location, and server ID) and then subjected to a keyed HMAC-SHA-256 transformation to produce a watermark payload that cannot be generated without the client's secret key. We further design FMGAN, a GAN-based robust image watermarking framework that embeds this transformed payload using a feature fusion module and a Mamba-guided linear attention mechanism. At the computation layer, we adopt a lattice-based zero-knowledge secure aggregation (LZKSA) protocol that verifies key correctness, L2 norm bounds, and cosine similarity constraints over committed gradients without revealing private updates. The RLWE-based design guarantees post-quantum security. Extensive experiments validate the complementary protection of the two layers under composite attack scenarios. To our knowledge, no prior verification workflow has jointly evaluated both layers in a hybrid, end-to-end trustworthy FL framework.

</details>


### [76] [MATEE: Efficiently Bridging the Semantic Gap in TrustZone via Arm Pointer Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20583)
*Shiqi Liu,Xiang Li,Jie Wang,Yongpeng Gao,Jiajin Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MATEE系统，利用Arm Pointer Authentication（PA）技术将请求与客户端应用（CA）身份绑定，以防御因Arm TrustZone安全世界与普通世界之间的语义差距（Semantic Gap）导致的语义差距漏洞（SGVs），在不修改现有CA和TA的情况下隔离不同CA的敏感数据，仅引入2.19%的运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Arm TrustZone将系统划分为安全世界和普通世界，但安全世界对普通世界的运行信息可见性有限，形成语义差距。具体而言，安全世界收到来自普通世界的数据请求时缺乏有效的用户身份认证，恶意CA可通过精心构造的请求参数欺骗安全世界中的TA，从而危害其他CA存储的敏感数据。因此需要系统性地防御此类语义差距漏洞。

Method: MATEE系统首先系统性地对语义差距漏洞（SGVs）进行分类，然后利用Arm Pointer Authentication（PA）技术将每个请求绑定到对应CA的身份上，在CA访问敏感数据时验证其身份，从而防止恶意请求伪造。特别地，MATEE在不修改现有CA和TA的前提下实现了不同CA敏感数据的隔离。

Result: 实验评估表明，MATEE能够成功防御各类语义差距漏洞，且仅引入极小的运行时开销（2.19%）。

Conclusion: MATEE为TEE环境提供了一种有效的语义差距漏洞防御方案，通过硬件级指针认证实现了请求身份绑定与验证，在保持兼容性和低开销的同时增强了TEE的安全性。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) employ hardware-based isolation mechanisms to safeguard the confidentiality and integrity of sensitive code and data. One such prevalent implementation is Arm TrustZone, which partitions the system into the secure and normal (non-secure) worlds. However, this partitioning results in the secure world having very limited visibility into the operating information of the normal world, creating a semantic gap between these two worlds. Specifically, the secure world lacks an effective user identity authentication when receiving data requests from the normal world. Consequently, malicious Client Applications (CAs) in the normal world can deceive Trusted Applications (TAs) in the secure world by utilizing elaborate request parameters, compromising the sensitive data stored by other CAs. We systematically classify these Semantic Gap Vulnerabilities (SGVs) and propose a mate system for the TEE called MATEE to defend against SGVs. MATEE utilizes Arm Pointer Authentication (PA) to bind each request to the corresponding CA's identity and then verifies the identity when the CA accesses sensitive data, thereby preventing malicious request forgery. In particular, MATEE isolates sensitive data of different CAs without modifying existing CAs and TAs. Our evaluation demonstrates that MATEE successfully defends against SGVs with a minimal runtime overhead (2.19%).

</details>


### [77] [More Granular, Less Trust: Enforcing Intra-Process Isolation with Arm CCA in an Untrusted Management Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20584)
*Shiqi Liu,Zhouqi Jiang,Jie Wang,Wei Zhou,Kun Sun,Zhaohui Chen,Yulai Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CCAegis，一种扩展Arm机密计算架构（CCA）的系统，通过在函数调用和返回点插入权限切换指令并利用颗粒保护表（GPT）调整权限，实现进程内敏感数据和操作与操作系统及进程内对手的隔离，仅信任Secure Monitor，最小化可信计算基（TCB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机密虚拟机（CVM）中，来自操作系统的特权攻击仍是重大威胁。现有细粒度隔离方案如SHELTER提供了进程级保护，但仍易受进程内攻击及OS与进程内对手的共谋影响。许多进程内隔离技术依赖OS管理和执行隔离域，导致较大的TCB。因此需要更细粒度、更少信任依赖的机密计算解决方案。

Method: CCAegis扩展Arm CCA架构，采用静态分析追踪敏感数据流并识别处理敏感数据的函数。在函数调用和返回点插入权限切换指令，通过Granule Protection Table（GPT）调整权限，确保仅指定函数能访问隔离数据。整个系统仅信任配置GPT和管理域切换的Secure Monitor。

Result: 在官方模拟器和真实开发板上实现并评估CCAegis。实验结果表明，CCAegis能有效隔离敏感数据和操作，在真实密码学工作负载下，性能开销相比原始版本为1.01x到1.43x。

Conclusion: CCAegis通过在CCA框架内实现仅依赖Secure Monitor的进程内隔离机制，成功抵御来自OS和进程内对手的攻击，显著缩小了TCB，同时保持了可接受的性能开销。

Abstract: With the increasing adoption of confidential computing, security-sensitive applications are often deployed in confidential virtual machines (CVMs), which reduce reliance on third-party cloud providers. However, privilege attacks originating from the OS remain a significant threat in these environments. Existing finer-grained isolation schemes, such as SHELTER, provide process-level protection but are still vulnerable to intraprocess attacks and potential collusion between the OS and intra-process adversaries. Many current intra-process isolation techniques continue to depend on the OS to manage and enforce isolation domains, leading to a large Trusted Computing Base (TCB). This gap highlights the need for more granular, less trust-dependent confidential computing solutions. In this paper, we present CCAegis, a system that extends the Arm Confidential Compute Architecture (CCA) to enforce intra-process isolation of sensitive data and operations, safeguarding them from both intraprocess adversaries and the OS. We employ static analysis to track the flow of sensitive data and identify functions that handle such data. Permission-switching instructions are inserted at the function call and return points, adjusting permissions via the Granule Protection Table (GPT) to ensure that only designated functions can access the isolated data. Notably, CCAegis places trust solely in the Secure Monitor, which configures the GPTs and manages domain switching, thereby minimizing the TCB. We implemented CCAegis on both an official emulator and a real development board to assess its performance. Our experimental results show that CCAegis effectively isolates sensitive data and operations, with performance overheads ranging from 1.01x to 1.43x compared to the original version across real-world cryptographic workloads.

</details>


### [78] [ARQ: Agentic CodeQL Query Refinement for C/C++ Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20637)
*Chunyi Wang,Yunfei Ke,Junfeng Yang,Yun-Yun Tsai,Penghui Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ARQ是一个智能体框架，通过合成C/C++程序执行结果与查询判定之间的分歧作为"ground truth"，自动优化CodeQL查询，无需标注数据集、提交历史或漏洞模板。实验表明，ARQ修复12个官方CodeQL查询后，在Juliet v1.3和FormAI v2数据集上真阳性检测提升了最多119.8%，精度不低于98.0%，并修复了三个遗留27个月的GitHub issue，在libpng和zlib中发现两个此前未知的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的静态分析工具（如CodeQL）在漏洞检测中仍存在大量误报（FP）和漏报（FN），而现有的查询精化方法依赖于标注数据集、提交历史或漏洞特定模板，这些资源往往稀缺或难以获得，限制了查询优化的效果和可扩展性。

Method: ARQ的核心洞察是：合成程序在运行时行为（执行）与查询判定不一致时即可暴露查询缺陷——程序确实脆弱但查询沉默则存在FN弱点，程序安全但查询触发则存在FP弱点。ARQ将执行结果作为ground truth，利用基于LLM的精化循环迭代修复查询弱点，反复合成新程序来验证修复效果，实现无需外部监督的自动化查询优化。

Result: 使用三种商业LLM（GPT-5.4、Claude-Sonnet-4.6、Gemini-3.5-flash）对12个官方CodeQL查询进行精化后，在Juliet v1.3和FormAI v2数据集上真阳性检测提升最多119.8%，精度维持不低于98.0%；成功修复了官方CodeQL仓库中三个最长搁置27个月的GitHub issue，并在libpng和zlib中发现了两个此前未知的真实漏洞。

Conclusion: ARQ验证了"执行-查询分歧"作为查询缺陷自动发现与修复信号的有效性，证明了一种无需人工标注或漏洞模板的查询精化路径能显著提升静态分析工具的实用效果，并具备发现真实软件漏洞的能力。

Abstract: Static analyzers have been widely adopted for vulnerability detection in C/C++ programs. Query-based static analyzers (e.g., CodeQL) encode vulnerable code patterns in detection queries and match them against source code. However, existing queries still suffer from false positives (FPs, incorrectly flagging benign code as vulnerable) and false negatives (FNs, missing real vulnerabilities). We present ARQ, an agentic framework that automatically refines C/C++ CodeQL queries using execution-grounded evidence from synthesized C/C++ programs. Our key insight is that a synthesized program exposes a query's weakness whenever its execution disagrees with the query's verdict. If the program is genuinely vulnerable but the query stays silent, the query has an FN weakness; if the program is safe but the query fires anyway, it has an FP weakness. ARQ then runs an LLM-based refinement loop that repairs the query using these disagreements as ground truth. Unlike previous query refining methods, ARQ requires no labeled datasets, no commit history, and no vulnerability-specific templates. We demonstrate the effectiveness of ARQ by refining 12 official CodeQL queries using three commercial LLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude-Sonnet-4.6, and Gemini-3.5-flash). We compare both ARQ-refined and original CodeQL queries on the Juliet v1.3 and FormAI v2 datasets and show that ARQ-refined queries detect substantially more true positives, by up to 119.8\%, with a Precision of at least 98.0\% throughout. ARQ successfully fixed three unresolved GitHub issues raised in the official CodeQL query repository that had remained open for as long as \textit{27 months}. The refined queries also exposed two previously undiscovered bugs in the real-world libraries libpng and zlib.

</details>


### [79] [The Claws in Plain Sight: Unauthorized Context Disclosure through LLM Agent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20658)
*Ben Dong,Zhonghao Guo,Tianyi Lu,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"Claw in Plain Sight"攻击：LLM agent在生成工具调用参数时，可通过"任务相关内容"将受保护属性包装为操作或程序所需，导致模型在合法参数中包含敏感信息。实验跨越5种模型配置、6种压力水平与4种隐私政策水平，会话级泄露率在20.8%至75.0%之间，更强的隐私指令能减少但无法一致消除泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent在构建工具调用参数时，通常会访问用户画像、对话历史、检索文档等上下文信息。然而，合法访问上下文信息并不等于被授权向任何目的或目的地传输这些信息。现有研究缺乏对"上下文到参数"这一边界上权威压力攻击的系统评估，需要探索模型在何种条件下会违反隐私政策将受保护属性写入工具参数。

Method: 构建受控合成基准，交叉6种压力水平与4种隐私政策水平，在5种DeepSeek和Claude模型配置上执行实验，产生120次调用。攻击核心在于将受保护属性嵌入"任务相邻"内容中，使其看起来在操作上或程序上被要求。

Result: 在完整压力-政策矩阵中，会话级泄露率在20.8%到75.0%之间。更强的隐私指令能降低总体泄露，但在不同模型间不能一致地消除泄露，表明提示级别的策略无法提供可移植的执法边界。

Conclusion: 该研究测量的是上下文到参数边界上的策略违规生成，而非完整网络窃取或真实用户泄露。研究动机在于需要在工具参数执行前进行目的感知和目的地感知的检查，以防范此类权威压力攻击。

Abstract: LLM agents routinely construct tool-call arguments from user profiles, conversation history, retrieved documents, and prior tool results. However, legitimate access to contextual information does not imply authorization to transmit that information for every purpose or destination. We present Claw in Plain Sight, an authority- pressure attack in which task-adjacent content frames protected attributes as operationally or procedurally required, causing a model to include them in otherwise valid generated arguments.
