We evaluate Claw in Plain Sight using a controlled synthetic benchmark that crosses six pressure levels with four privacy-policy levels across five DeepSeek and Claude model configurations, producing 120 calls. Across the complete pressure-policy matrix, session-level disclosure rates range from 20.8% to 75.0% among the tested models. Stronger privacy instructions reduce aggregate disclosure but do not eliminate it consistently across models, showing that prompt-level policies do not provide a portable enforcement boundary. Our experiments use only synthetic profiles and capture proposed arguments locally; they measure policy-violating generation at the context-to-argument boundary, not completed network exfiltration or leakage from deployed users. These findings motivate purpose- and destination-aware inspection of generated tool arguments before execution.

</details>


### [80] [Bootstrapping Mutual Attestation with Kleene's Second Recursion Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20671)
*Takuma Imamura*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解决无中心可信第三方场景下相互证明（mutual attestation）的"参考值自举问题"（reference-value bootstrapping problem）的新方法。该方法将自举问题建模为相互不动点方程组，利用Kleene第二递归定理求解，使每个节点仅凭内置数据即可重建对端节点的精确源代码并计算其参考度量值。作者分别开发了PyReflect（Python转译器）和NixReflect（Nix转译器）作为工具，在TPM和AWS Nitro Enclaves环境中实现了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无中心可信操作者的去中心化网络中，节点间的相互证明要求每个节点持有对端节点的期望代码度量值（参考值）。若将这些参考值以朴素方式互相嵌入各节点代码中，会导致无限回归问题。现有解决方案依赖可信第三方（TTP）、外部提供的参考值或特定架构的度量机制，均存在局限性。作者希望提出一种架构无关、无需TTP或外部参考值的通用解决方案。

Method: 本文将参考值自举问题形式化为相互不动点方程组，并利用Kleene第二递归定理（Kleene's second recursion theorem）求解该方程组。构造出的节点能够内置数据即可重建对端节点的精确源代码。对于直接度量源码文件的情况（如Python脚本），节点通过对重建源码应用度量函数直接获得对端参考值；对于度量构建镜像的情况（如AWS Nitro Enclaves），节点从重建源码可复现地重建对端镜像并推导出其参考测量值。作者为此开发了PyReflect（Python转译器）和NixReflect（Nix转译器）两个工具，并分别实现了TPM相互证明和双Nitro Enclaves相互复制参考PCR的PoC。

Result: 作者通过PyReflect实现了基于TPM的相互证明概念验证，通过NixReflect实现了两个AWS Nitro Enclaves仅凭内置数据即可相互复现对方参考PCR值的概念验证。该方案架构无关，不依赖TTP或外部提供的参考值，且可与现有证明栈无缝兼容。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上证明了可以通过递归定理彻底解决相互证明中的无限回归问题，提供了泛化的、架构无关的解决方案。其创新之处在于用递归理论而非传统信任链思维来突破信任自举的困境，为去中心化网络中的设备认证提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Mutual attestation among nodes with no central trusted operator requires each node to hold reference values (expected code measurements) for its peers. The naïve approach of mutually embedding these reference values in the nodes' code leads to an infinite regress. We call the problem of resolving this infinite regress the reference-value bootstrapping problem for mutual attestation. Existing solutions avoid this regress by relying on a trusted third party (TTP), externally supplied reference values, or architecture-specific measurement mechanisms. We instead express the bootstrapping problem as a system of mutual fixed-point equations and solve it by Kleene's second recursion theorem. The construction produces nodes that mutually reference one another's code and reconstruct every peer's exact source from built-in data alone. When a deployed source file is measured directly, as with a Python script, a node obtains the peer's reference value by applying the measurement function directly to the reconstructed source. When a built image is measured, as with AWS Nitro Enclaves, a node instead reproducibly rebuilds the peer's image from the reconstructed source and derives its reference measurement. For the first case, we develop PyReflect, a Python transpiler, and use it to implement a TPM mutual-attestation PoC. For the second, we develop NixReflect, a Nix transpiler, and use it in a PoC in which two Nitro Enclaves reproduce each other's reference PCRs from built-in data alone. Our solution is architecture-independent, requires neither a TTP nor externally supplied reference values, and works with existing attestation stacks unchanged.

</details>


### [81] [The Rising Cost of Trust: Practitioners' Trust Signals, Controls, and Responses in the Software Supply Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20675)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Siri Paidipalli,Laurie Williams,Christian Kästner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对38位从业者的半结构化访谈，研究发现软件供应链中的信任正在侵蚀，从业者通过自动化验证、委托信任决策给"守护者"或考虑完全退出供应链来应对日益高昂的信任成本。研究提供了以信任视角理解供应链动态的概念框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链日益复杂，AI正在重塑其威胁格局（如AI生成依赖的质量问题）。从信任视角看，供应链信任侵蚀的后果严重，但缺乏从业者信任的实证基线数据。

Method: 对38位从业者（包括行业从业者和开源从业者）进行半结构化访谈，聚焦揭示偏好（已采纳的控制措施）而非陈述态度，使用基于社会科学信任概念的主题分析对数据进行分析。

Result: 研究发现信任正在侵蚀且代价高昂：有意识的从业者正在积累控制措施。为应对上升的信任成本，从业者采取自动化验证、将信任决策委托给"守护者"，或考虑完全退出软件供应链。

Conclusion: 以信任视角理解软件供应链动态，提供了必要的词汇和概念（如信任守护者、系统信任、信号）来塑造未来干预措施，以实现具有适当信任水平的良性供应链运作。

Abstract: The software supply chain is becoming more complex, and AI is reshaping its threat landscape, e.g., raising concerns about the quality of AI-generated dependencies. Seen through the lens of trust, the stakes of eroding trust in the software supply chain are high, yet we lack an empirical baseline on practitioners' trust. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in taking informed actions as trust in the software supply chain evolves, through an interview study with 38 practitioners. We conducted semi-structured interviews with industry and open-source practitioners, focusing on their revealed preferences (the controls they adopted) rather than their stated attitudes, and analyzed the data using thematic analysis grounded in established trust concepts from the social sciences. We find that trust is eroding, which is becoming costly: aware practitioners are accumulating controls. To cope with the rising cost of trust, practitioners automate verification, delegate trust decisions to guardians, or consider exiting the software supply chain entirely. Understanding software supply chain dynamics through the lens of trust provides the vocabulary and concepts (e.g., guardians of trust, system trust, signals) to shape future interventions for a well-functioning supply chain with appropriate levels of trust.

</details>


### [82] [The Software Supply Chain as a Market for Lemons: A Multivocal Review of Trust Signal Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20678)
*Ranindya Paramitha,Christian Kästner,Laurie Williams*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过多元文献综述（252个Google搜索来源和870个Reddit帖子）调查了开源依赖采纳信任信号（如下载量、贡献者活跃度）的可靠性，发现这些廉价信号在三种力量下崩溃：对抗性操纵、与合法行为难以区分的刷量行为，以及非对抗性的AI驱动通胀。文献记录的反应多为建议而非实际行动（54.6%仅提供建议），且提出的替代方案（替换或聚合信号）同样可被操纵。作者主张强制性密码学认证等更昂贵的信号应成为默认要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件从业人员评估开源依赖时依赖下载量、星标、贡献者活跃度等廉价信任信号来替代直接代码审查，但现有研究仅记录了单一信号的操纵问题，缺乏对所有依赖采纳信号崩溃的整体景观以及生态系统的应对反应的系统理解。

Method: 采用多元文献综述（multivocal review）方法，对252个Google搜索来源和870个Reddit线程进行系统收集、编码和定性分析，识别信任信号崩溃的机制及文献中记录的应对措施。

Result: 发现廉价信任信号的崩溃源于三重力量：对抗性操纵、与合法行为无法区分的刷量技术，以及非对抗性的AI驱动通胀。文献记录的反应以建议为主而无实际行动（54.6%），提出的替代方案（用一个廉价信号替换另一个或聚合多个信号）同样可被游戏化。非对抗性通胀缺乏任何实际行动变化的记录。

Conclusion: 已知补救措施与实际实践之间的差距指向"柠檬市场"（市场失灵）：当伪造信号成本低于真正获得信号时，好坏依赖变得无法区分。依赖个人从业者验证廉价信号不可持续，应强制推行密码学认证等更昂贵的信号，使其成为所有人的默认而非少数人的自愿选择。

Abstract: Practitioners evaluating open-source dependencies rely on cheap trust signals, e.g., stars, download counts, and contributor activity, as substitutes for direct code inspection, assuming those signals reflect genuine trustworthiness. Prior work has documented individual signal gaming, but the landscape of collapses across all dependency-adoption signals, as well as the ecosystem's response, remains unexplored. The goal of this study is to aid software practitioners in understanding the reliability of dependency adoption trust signals, such as download counts and contributor activity, by conducting a multivocal review of 252 Google Search sources and 870 Reddit threads. After coding the corpora, we find that cheap trust signals collapse under three simultaneous forces: adversarial manipulation, gaming techniques indistinguishable from legitimate behavior, and non-adversarial AI-driven inflation. The documented responses are more advice than actual action: 54.6% of Google Search sources contain advice on what practitioners should do, with no actual action taken. Responses proposed substituting one cheap signal for another or aggregating multiple signals, which are now also gameable. Non-adversarial inflation, i.e., degradation caused by the emergence of legitimate AI tooling, lacks documented actual behavior change in either corpus. The gap between known remedy and actual practice points toward a market for lemons: when faking signals costs less than earning them, good and bad dependencies become indistinguishable. Relying on individual practitioners to verify the cheap signals is not sustainable. Costlier signals, such as cryptographic attestation, should be made mandatory so that they become the default for all, not a voluntary choice for the few.

</details>


### [83] [Enabling Threshold Custody for the Lightning Network with Nested Threshold Multi-Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20705)
*Paul Gerhart,Nadav Kohen,Jesse Posner,Matias Furszyfer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"嵌套阈值多重签名"这一新密码学原语，并基于其构建了Iceberg构造——首个嵌套阈值MuSig2签名方案。Iceberg允许闪电通道一方作为t-of-n阈值组运行，同时对外仍表现为标准MuSig2参与者，从而无需修改比特币、闪电协议或对手方即可单方面部署阈值托管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闪电网络保障了数亿美元资产，但通道端点依赖易受攻击的单一在线密钥。尽管阈值签名已常规用于保护链上比特币，但尚未在闪电通道中实际部署，原因在于将一方阈值化需要在既有两方MuSig2协议内部嵌套阈值签名方案，而不改变其随机数交换或消息流程。

Method: 引入嵌套阈值多重签名（nested threshold multi-signatures）这一新原语，形式化描述在一个多方签名协议内部阈值化单一参与者的安全性。作为该原语的实例化，提出Iceberg构造方案，实现嵌套阈值MuSig2签名：一方以t-of-n阈值组运行，但外部表现为标准MuSig2参与者。

Result: 完成了Iceberg的安全性证明，将其原型集成到生产级闪电节点中并进行了性能基准测试。测量表明，阈值化闪电通道仅引入适度开销，容忍一个腐败成员的阈值组仍能维持未修改端点93%以上的支付吞吐量。

Conclusion: Iceberg证明了在现有闪电网络上可单方面部署阈值托管，无需对底层协议或对手方做出任何改动，为加强闪电通道安全提供了实用、高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The Bitcoin Lightning Network secures hundreds of millions of dollars, yet channel endpoints rely on vulnerable single online keys. Although threshold signatures are routinely used to protect on-chain Bitcoin, no practical deployment has been possible for Lightning channels. This is because thresholdizing a Lightning party requires nesting a threshold signature scheme inside of an established two-party MuSig2 protocol without altering its nonce exchange or message flow.
  In this work, we resolve this limitation by formalizing nested threshold multi-signatures, a new cryptographic primitive for thresholdizing one participant inside a multi-signature protocol. As an instance of this primitive, we present Iceberg, the first construction for nested threshold MuSig2 signatures. Iceberg enables one side of a Lightning channel to operate as a $t$-of-$n$ threshold group while appearing to the counterparty as a standard MuSig2 participant. As a result, threshold custody can be deployed unilaterally on today's Lightning Network without requiring any modifications to Bitcoin, the Lightning protocol, or channel counterparties.
  We prove the security of Iceberg, integrate a prototype into a production Lightning node, and benchmark its performance. Our measurements show that thresholdizing a Lightning channel incurs only modest overhead, since a threshold group tolerating one corrupted member sustains over $93\%$ of the payment throughput of an unmodified endpoint.

</details>


### [84] [Privacy-Preserving Object Detection for Vision Transformer-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20712)
*Homare Sueyoshi,Kiyoshi Nishikawa,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一种基于感知加密的目标检测方法，利用Vision Transformer（ViT）的嵌入结构和带密钥的域适应技术，在保护测试图像敏感视觉信息的同时，实现与无保护模型几乎相同的检测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对视觉信息保护的研究主要集中在图像分类任务上，而目标检测任务中的敏感视觉信息保护尚未被探索。随着目标检测模型在真实世界中的广泛应用，测试图像中可能包含敏感信息，亟需在目标检测场景下实现视觉信息保护。

Method: 提出第一个使用感知加密的目标检测方法。该方法利用Vision Transformer（ViT）的嵌入结构特性，结合带密钥的域适应技术（domain adaptation with keys），使得加密后的图像仍能保持模型可用的特征表示，同时保护敏感视觉信息不被泄露。

Result: 在基于ViT的目标检测模型ViTdet上验证了方法的有效性，在检测精度和视觉信息保护两个维度均取得了良好效果，实现了与无保护模型几乎相同的检测精度。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将感知加密引入目标检测任务，证明了通过ViT嵌入结构和密钥域适应技术可以有效平衡目标检测精度与视觉信息保护之间的权衡，为隐私保护下的目标检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose a novel object detection method that enables us to protect sensitive visual information of test images. Previous studies considering visual information protection focus on image classification tasks. This paper proposes an object detection method using perceptual encryption for the first time. The proposed method can achieve almost the same accuracy as that of models without any protection by utilizing the embedding structure of the Vision Transformer (ViT) and a domain adaptation technique with keys. In experiments, the effectiveness of the proposed method is verified in terms of accuracy and visual protection under the use of ViTdet, which is a ViT-based object detection model.

</details>


### [85] [Uncovering and Understanding Hidden Dependencies in the LLM API Reseller Ecosystem via Prefix-Cache Side Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20732)
*Zimo Ji,Xin Wei,Congying Xu,Wenyuan Jiang,Xin Yang,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CacheTracer，首个基于API-only测量的方法来揭示LLM API转售商之间隐藏的依赖关系。通过利用前缀缓存复用作为侧信道，CacheTracer在39个转售商端点上发送110万次请求，发现37.1%的端点对共享缓存可达性，依赖结构深达七层，揭示了看似独立的转售商之间存在的深层次隐藏依赖，可能形成巨大的安全影响半径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM API转售商已成为现代LLM服务的重—访问层，但多级转售形成了不透明的供应链：用户的请求可能经过未披露的上游转售商，每个环节都可查看或修改提示和响应，导致生态系统级别的机密性和完整性风险。现有研究仅审计单个转售商，无法洞察转售商之间隐藏的依赖关系。因此需要一种能够测量这种隐藏依赖关系的方法。

Method: 核心洞察是利用前缀缓存复用作为侧信道，通过"缓存可达关系"（cache-reach relations）来测量依赖。CacheTracer包含两个原语：Flood（通过一个端点填充新鲜缓存状态）和Prove（探测另一个端点是否能复用该缓存，同时排除由探测本身创建的缓存命中）。基于这两个原语构建测量方法，在39个转售商端点上进行大规模真实世界测量。

Result: 在39个转售商端点、636个端点对上发送了110万次API请求。结果显示：37.1%的测量对表现出共享缓存可达性；包含关系的顺序跨越七层；一个缓存可达被至少31个其他节点包含。恢复出的结构是模型特定的。通过真实世界一致性检查和受控实验验证了CacheTracer的可靠性和准确性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了看似独立的API转售商之间存在大量隐藏依赖关系。这种深层次且集中的依赖会产生巨大的潜在影响半径：沿公共上游路径的机密性或完整性故障可能影响多个下游转售商的用户，对整个LLM生态系统构成安全威胁。

Abstract: LLM API resellers have become an important access layer to modern LLM services. However, multi-level resale creates an opaque supply chain: a user's request may traverse undisclosed upstream resellers, each of which can inspect or modify prompts and responses, inducing ecosystem-level confidentiality and integrity risks. Existing studies audit individual resellers, but provide little visibility into hidden dependencies across resellers. We present CacheTracer, the first API-only measurement of such hidden dependencies. Our key insight is to exploit prefix-cache reuse as a side channel to measure dependency via cache-reach relations. CacheTracer operationalizes this insight with two primitives: Flood populates fresh cache state through one endpoint, and Prove probes whether another can reuse it while excluding probe-created hits.
  We then conduct a real-world measurement study with CacheTracer on 39 reseller endpoints, sending 1.1 million API requests across 636 endpoint pairs. Our measurements reveal a deep, concentrated cache-reach structure: 37.1% of measured pairs exhibit shared cache reach, the containment order spans seven layers, and one cache reach is contained within at least 31 of other nodes. We further find that the recovered structure is model-specific. We also evaluate the validity of CacheTracer through both real-world consistency checks and controlled experiments. The results show its high reliability and accuracy. These findings reveal substantial hidden dependencies among seemingly independent API resellers. Such deep and concentrated dependencies can create a large potential blast radius, where a confidentiality or integrity failure along a common upstream path may affect users across multiple downstream resellers.

</details>


### [86] [Beyond Explicit Generators: Distribution-Free Linear-Decomposition Attacks on Public-Key Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20798)
*Ziyan Chen,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种线性分解攻击框架，证明在公开可采样线性密钥传输下，若加密分布的有效维数（采样轨道维数）为多项式级别，则方案与IND-CPA安全不相容。该框架应用于2024年基于扭曲-斜群环的概率公钥加密方案，仅利用公开采样-求值预言机即可实现明文恢复和恒定IND-CPA优势，实验验证了线性传输和端到端恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性分解攻击可以在不恢复秘密代数作用的情况下攻破公钥方案，但现有研究通常假设目标公开状态位于已知线性张成空间中，且敌手目标多为恢复完整代数张成。本文研究更现实的场景：敌手仅使用与诚实参与者相同的公开采样-求值预言机，诱导分布是任意的，目标是攻击未来密文而非恢复完整代数张成空间。

Method: 1) 将公开配对样本建模为固定秘密线性传输下的分布；2) 定义采样轨道维数作为加密分布的有效维度；3) 证明分布无关的一次性恢复定理、未来密文覆盖的高概率证书，以及最优采样张成复杂度m*_span(r,ε,δ)=Θ((r+log(1/δ))/ε)；4) 基于上述结果导出通用不可能性定理；5) 将该框架应用于2024年扭曲-斜群环概率PKE的具体攻击。

Result: 证明公开可采样线性密钥传输在多项式采样轨道维数下与IND-CPA安全不相容。对2024年扭曲-斜群环PKE，仅利用独立生成的公开协议样本即可发起采样器线性攻击，实现明文恢复和恒定IND-CPA优势。实验验证了线性传输性质和端到端恢复，并显示高未来密文覆盖率可能先于完整代数张成的恢复。

Conclusion: 本文建立了线性分解攻击的形式化理论框架，给出了通用不可能性定理：任何具有多项式采样轨道维数的公开可采样线性密钥传输方案在传输值决定解密负载时都不可能是IND-CPA安全的。该框架成功运用于2024年的具体PKE方案，展示了基于采样器而非完整代数恢复的攻击路径的有效性。这一结果表明，高未来密文覆盖能力本身就可能构成安全威胁，而不必先恢复完整代数张成空间。

Abstract: Linear-decomposition attacks can break public-key schemes without recovering the secret algebraic action: when a target public state lies in a known linear span, its decomposition coefficients transfer through the unknown action to reveal the shared value. We study a setting in which the adversary uses only the public sampling-and-evaluation oracle available to honest participants, the induced distribution is arbitrary, and the goal is to attack future ciphertexts rather than recover the full algebraic span.
  We model public paired samples under a fixed secret linear transport and define the sampled-orbit dimension as the effective dimension of the encryption distribution. We prove distribution-free one-shot recovery, a high-probability certificate for the future-ciphertext coverage of a sampled span, and the optimal sampled-span complexity $m^\star_{\mathrm{span}}(r,\varepsilon,δ) =Θ((r+\log(1/δ))/\varepsilon)$. These results yield a generic impossibility theorem: publicly samplable linear key transport with polynomial sampled-orbit dimension is incompatible with IND--CPA security when the transported value determines the decryption payload.
  We apply the framework to the 2024 probabilistic PKE from twisted--skew group rings. Its underlying Computational Twisted--Skew Problem admits a sampler-only linear attack using independently generated public protocol samples, yielding plaintext recovery and constant IND--CPA advantage. Experiments verify the linear transport and end-to-end recovery, and show that high future-ciphertext coverage may precede recovery of the full algebraic span.

</details>


### [87] [GhostTac: Manipulating Tactile Sensors without Physical Contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20817)
*Kun Wang,Xuancun Lu,Ruochen Zhou,Kai Wang,Tongjun Ye,Yihao Shao,Chen Yan,Xiaoyu Ji,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GhostTac，这是首个通过电磁干扰（EMI）对机器人触觉传感器进行无接触攻击的方法。它利用非线性整流和有限带宽放大特性，将精心设计的EMI信号转换为持续的直流偏移，绕过传感器板载滤波，实现对触觉输出的精细可控篡改，可诱导机器人产生危险行为（如过度施力）。在10个传感器模块和两只灵巧手上共15种不同型号的触觉传感器上验证了一致有效性，并通过触觉抓取、滑移检测和材料分类三个案例展示了实际危害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器是现代机器人系统的核心组件，但其物理层安全性长期被忽视。现有研究未系统探索针对触觉传感器的电磁干扰攻击向量，需要一个系统性的方法来证明和揭示这一安全漏洞。

Method: GhostTac通过以下方式实现攻击：(1)利用传感器非线性的整流特性，将EMI信号转换为直流偏置；(2)利用有限带宽放大电路，使攻击信号绕过板载滤波机制；(3)通过精确控制干扰信号的空间分布和幅度，在目标位置产生稳定且可控的测量偏差，从而实现对手触觉输出的精细操纵。

Result: 在10个传感器模块和两只灵巧手（覆盖15种不同类型触觉传感器）上均一致有效。三个实际机器人任务案例（触觉抓取、滑移检测、材料分类）展示了攻击对真实任务的破坏性影响。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一个全新的针对机器人触觉传感的物理攻击向量，表明现有的电磁干扰防护需纳入机器人触觉感知系统的安全设计中。这是该攻击类型首次被系统性地提出和验证。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are integral to modern robotic systems, enabling robots to perceive and interact with the physical environment through tactile feedback. However, the physical-layer security of tactile sensors has received little attention. We present GhostTac, the first contactless attack, to the best of our knowledge, that manipulates tactile sensing through electromagnetic interference (EMI). GhostTac exploits nonlinear rectification and limited-bandwidth amplification, converting carefully crafted EMI signals into persistent DC offsets that bypass onboard filtering and induce stable measurement deviations. It enables fine-grained, controllable manipulation of sensor outputs by shaping the spatial distribution and magnitude of interference at targeted locations. Such manipulation can induce harmful robot behaviors, including excessive force that may damage objects or injure people. We evaluate GhostTac on 10 sensor modules and two dexterous hands, covering 15 tactile sensors of different types, and demonstrate consistent effectiveness across all tested devices. Three case studies involving tactile grasping, slip detection, and material classification further illustrate its practical impact on real robotic tasks. These findings reveal a new physical attack vector against tactile sensing in robotic systems.

</details>


### [88] [On the Additive FFT Techniques over Binary Extension Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20855)
*Susanta Samanta,Mohammadtaghi Badakhshan,Guang Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于Bailey四步FFT算法的启发，开发了二进制扩展域仿射子空间上多项式求值的加法FFT（AFFT）技术。核心洞见是利用子空间消失多项式的Taylor展开，将AFFT分解为与矩阵行列相关的独立子AFFT。提出了通用基AFFT，并针对Cantor特殊基设计了两种专门算法，其中一种需要恰好1/2·n·log₂n次乘法。实验表明该算法在42种配置中的37种上优于LCH AFFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的加法FFT算法在二进制扩展域多项式求值中存在计算效率和结构灵活性的局限。作者希望借鉴Bailey四步FFT的矩阵分解思想，开发更通用且更高效的AFFT框架，以统一不同基和维度划分下的算法设计，并识别哪些专门化策略能带来实际收益。

Method: 1) 提出一般基AFFT框架，适用于任意有序基和任意维度拆分，作为衡量专门化收益的基线；2) 将框架专门化到Cantor特殊基，得到两种算法：第一种支持AFFT维度的任意分解，利用Cantor特殊基结构使Taylor展开阶段无需有限域乘法；第二种采用保持子空间多项式二项式形式的分解，需要精确的1/2·n·log₂n次乘法和由m二进制表示决定的闭式加法计数。采用完全递归结构以提供内存局部性。3) 引入部分Cantor特殊基的概念，分析von zur Gathen-Gerhard算法和通用基AFFT在加法和乘法次数上优于首个Gao-Mateer算法的参数区间。

Result: 实验结果表明，新算法在两个硬件平台的42种配置中有37种快于Cantor特殊基上的LCH AFFT。性能优势源于其完全递归结构带来的内存局部性设计，以及避免了单独的基转换和求值阶段。此外，在部分Cantor特殊基下，von zur Gathen-Gerhard算法和通用基AFFT在特定参数区间内比首个Gao-Mateer算法需要更少的加法和乘法运算。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的AFFT分析框架，通过矩阵分解视角将Bailey四步FFT的思想成功移植到二进制扩展域的加法FFT中。Cantor特殊基下的专门化算法在计算复杂度和实际性能上均展现出优势，同时部分Cantor特殊基的形式化分析为不同参数条件下的算法选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Motivated by Bailey's four-step FFT algorithm (1989), we develop additive FFT techniques for polynomial evaluation over affine subspaces of binary extension fields. Our key insight is that the Taylor expansion with respect to vanishing polynomials of subspaces provides a structural counterpart to Bailey's matrix formulation. It decomposes an additive FFT (AFFT) into independent sub-AFFTs associated with the columns and rows of a matrix. We first present a general-basis AFFT that applies to any ordered basis and any split of the dimension, providing a unified baseline for measuring the gains from specialization. We then specialize the framework to the Cantor special basis and obtain two AFFT algorithms. The first supports an arbitrary decomposition of the AFFT dimension and exploits the Cantor special basis structure to perform the Taylor expansion stage without finite field multiplications. The second uses a decomposition that preserves the binomial form of the relevant subspace polynomials. It requires exactly $\frac{1}{2}n\log_2 n$ multiplications, together with a closed-form addition count determined by the binary representation of $m$. Our implementation results show that this algorithm is faster than the LCH AFFT over a Cantor special basis in 37 of the 42 configurations tested across two hardware platforms. This performance advantage stems from its fully recursive structure, which provides memory locality by design and avoids separate basis-conversion and evaluation stages. Finally, in a separate analysis, we formalize the notion of partial Cantor special bases and identify parameter regimes in which both the von zur Gathen-Gerhard algorithm and our general-basis AFFT require fewer additions and multiplications than the first Gao-Mateer algorithm.

</details>


### [89] [Vibe Coding and Web Application Security: A Twin-Prompt Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20963)
*Darko Andročec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨在自然语言提示中显式添加安全需求是否提升LLM生成Web应用的安全性。利用同一智能体编码助手和同一模型，为六个功能各异的Web应用各生成两个变体（基线A与安全感知B），通过静态、依赖、动态及人工四种技术分析共85个候选发现确认75个。结果表明安全感知变体在每个应用中确认发现更少（24比51），且无严重或高危问题；最严重问题仅靠人工测试发现。鉴于语料规模小且各变体仅生成一次，研究定位为初步探索，报告描述性观察而非统计显著效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益能够从自然语言提示生成完整的Web应用，但显式请求安全最佳实践是否能改善生成结果的安全性仍不清楚。

Method: 选取六个功能不同的Web应用，每个应用生成两个提示变体（基线A和安全感知B，除附加安全需求章节外完全一致）；由同一智能体编码助手和同一模型在单轮非迭代生成中产出全部12个程序；随后采用静态分析、依赖分析、动态分析和人工测试四种方法进行评估。

Result: 共85个候选发现中确认75个；安全感知变体在每个应用中的确认发现均少于基线（24对51），且不包含任何严重或高危问题；最严重的发现仅由人工测试检测到。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明在提示中显式加入安全需求能降低生成Web应用中的安全问题数量；但由于语料规模小且每个变体仅生成一次，结果以描述性观察呈现，需扩展至更多模型和多次运行以建立统计效应。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete web applications from natural-language prompts, raising the question of whether explicitly requesting security best practice improves the result. We study six functionally distinct web applications, each generated in two prompt variants that are identical except for an appended security-requirements section: a baseline (A) and a security-aware (B) variant. All twelve programs were produced by the same agentic coding assistant and the same model version in a single, non-iterative generation round, and were then analyzed with static, dependency, dynamic and manual techniques, yielding 75 confirmed findings out of 85 candidates. The security-aware variant produced fewer confirmed findings in every application (24 versus 51) and contained no Critical or High issues; the most severe finding was detected only by manual testing. Because the corpus is small and each variant was generated once, we report descriptive observations rather than statistically established effects, and position the work as a preliminary study whose pipeline is being scaled to multiple models and repeated runs.

</details>


### [90] [Structured but Fragile: On the Limits of LLMs in Cybersecurity Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20966)
*Pasquale Malacaria,Yunxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型（LLMs）在网络安全防御决策中的结构化推理能力。通过在真实威胁场景的攻击图上进行防御策略选择实验，并与博弈论优化基线对比，发现LLMs在攻击图结构明确时能产生接近最优的策略，但其能力脆弱：随图复杂度增加而下降，对提示措辞高度敏感，且其评估器对策略的评分与形式化风险呈非单调关系。LLM生成的求解器虽能恢复正确的高层公式，但扩展性远不如专用求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地被用于网络安全工作流中，但我们尚不清楚它们是否真正具备结构化的安全推理能力，还是仅依赖表面线索和先验知识。理解这一问题对于AI辅助安全决策系统的设计和评估至关重要。

Method: 在从真实威胁场景（勒索软件、供应链攻击、云滥用、Kubernetes攻击、POS恶意软件、ICS/OT入侵）构建的攻击图上，给定预算约束，让LLMs选择安全控制以最小化攻击者成功概率。将LLM策略与游戏论优化基线进行比较，同时通过提示变化、重新标记实验和LLM求解器生成等方式系统性地探测其推理鲁棒性。

Result: LLMs展现出条件性能力：当明确的攻击图结构被提供时，常能产生接近优化基线的连贯策略。但能力脆弱：策略质量随图的复杂性增加而下降；对提示措辞高度敏感，微小的提示变化能显著改变排名；将差策略重新标记为"最优"会大幅提升其评估得分；形式化风险与LLM判断之间呈非单调关系；LLM生成的求解器虽然公式正确，但扩展性远差于专用求解器。

Conclusion: LLMs在受控表示下能近似结构化网络安全推理，但无法稳健地应用这一能力。这对AI辅助安全决策支持系统的设计与评估具有重要启示，表明需要更加审慎地对待LLM在安全领域的输出，并设计更鲁棒的评估机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in cybersecurity workflows, yet it remains unclear whether they can perform structured security reasoning or merely rely on superficial cues and prior knowledge. We study this question in the context of defence selection over attack graphs derived from real-world threat scenarios, including ransomware, supply-chain compromise, cloud abuse, Kubernetes attacks, POS malware, and ICS/OT intrusion. Given a budget constraint, LLMs must select security controls to minimise attacker success. We compare their strategies against each other and against a game-theoretic optimization baseline used as a normative reference for structured reasoning. Our results show that LLMs exhibit conditional competence. When explicit attack-graph structure is provided, they often produce coherent strategies close to the optimization baseline. However, their capabilities are fragile. LLM behaviour becomes increasingly fragile with graph complexity and is highly sensitive to framing. Small prompt changes can substantially alter rankings, and merely relabeling a poor strategy as ``optimal'' dramatically improves its evaluation. We further observe a non-monotonic relationship between formal risk and LLM judgement: strategies closest to the optimum are not necessarily ranked highest by LLM evaluators. To further probe reasoning ability, we ask LLMs to generate solvers for the same optimization problem. While the generated implementations recover the correct high-level formulation, they scale poorly compared to a purpose-built solver. Overall, our findings show that LLMs can approximate structured cybersecurity reasoning under controlled representations, but do not apply it robustly. This has important implications for the design and evaluation of AI-assisted security decision-support systems.

</details>


### [91] [$Z^2$-ACT: End-to-End Verifiable Agentic Intent Control for Open 6G RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21049)
*Sunder Ali Khowaja,Kapal Dev,George C. Alexandropoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出零知识可审计控制与零信任可验证智能体意图架构（Z²-ACT），将智能体协调、形式化意图约束、零信任提示验证和密码学问责四种原语集成至6G开放无线接入网的非实时与近实时RIC中，并利用ColO-RAN公开数据集验证了其在执行过滤、攻击韧性与延迟开销方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式和分解式6G无线接入网络需支持多厂商共存，但AI辅助控制回路在并发运营商意图和不可信模型输入下必须保持安全、可验证和可审计。现有研究各自孤立地处理智能体协调、形式化意图约束、零信任提示验证和密码学问责，单独使用时无法实现部署前安全保障、持续语义验证和跨域审计的完整性。

Method: 提出Z²-ACT架构：(1) 将操作员目标编码为类型化意图契约（Typed Intent Contracts）；(2) 对LLM输入实施实用的对抗性意图检查；(3) 技能序列仅在自管理门控条件满足后才释放；(4) 每次成功提交记录为带零知识证明的绑定承诺。架构集成于非实时和近实时RIC中，非实时路径使用实时LLM翻译操作员意图，近实时控制基于公开KPM序列进行trace-driven仿真。

Result: 在ColO-RAN公开测量数据上与针对性的消融实验及传统强化学习基线对比，报告了翻译准确率、无效或幻觉契约率、非实时延迟及对抗/误导意图下的行为表现。结果显示在执行过滤和攻击韧性方面有所提升，延迟和信令开销在近实时时延包络内保持适度。

Conclusion: Z²-ACT通过集成四种安全原语，在近实时控制预算内实现了对执行动作的有效过滤和攻击韧性的改进，同时保持了可接受的延迟与信令开销，为多厂商6G RAN中安全、可审计的AI控制闭环提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the progression in open and disaggregated 6G radio access networks, it is expected that the system will be able to host multi-vendors. In order to host multi-vendors, it is essential that AI-assisted control loops remain safe, verifiable, and auditable under concurrent operator intents and untrusted model inputs. The existing studies address the agentic coordination, formal intent constraints, zero-trust prompt verification and cryptographic accountability in isolation, which leaves pre-realization safety, continuous semantic verification and cross-domain audit incomplete when used individually. In this regard, we propose zero-knowledge auditable control and zero-trust verifiable agentic intent architecture ($Z^2$-ACT), which integrates the aforementioned four primitives across the non-real-time and near-real-time RICs. We encode the typed Intent Contracts as operator goals while the large language model inputs are only admitted after a practical adversarial intent check. The skill sequences in the proposed study are released only when a self-management gate is satisfied while every successful commit is recorded as a binding commitment with a zero-knowledge proof. Our experimental evaluation on public ColO-RAN measurements compares the full architecture against targeted ablations and a conventional reinforcement-learning baseline. A live large language model is used in the non-real-time path to translate operator intents into Intent Contracts; we report translation accuracy, the rate of invalid or hallucinated contracts, non-real-time latency, and behavior under adversarial or misleading intents. Near-real-time control remains trace-driven on the public KPM sequences. Results indicate improved actuation filtering and attack resilience at modest latency and signaling cost inside the near-real-time envelope.

</details>


### [92] [ClawSentry: A Progressive Multi-Tier Security Monitor for Safeguarding Autonomous LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21101)
*Kai Wang,Zeming Wei,BiaoJie Zeng,Chang Jin,An Wang,Xiaokun Luan,Zhixiao Lin,Jingjing Qu,Xia Hu,Xingcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ClawSentry，一个框架无关的开源代理安全监督网关，基于"代理风险渐进式"威胁模型（四个控制环路位点），通过首用技能包审查（FSPR）、三层渐进决策引擎和会话级防绕过机制，将恶意技能注入的上下文攻击成功率从39.55%降至2.61%，同时几乎不影响正常技能的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理从对话转向执行代码、读取文件和编排外部工具，单个被恶意第三方技能劫持的代理可导致数据泄露、权限提升或级联式入侵。现有安全防护通常局限于单一生命周期边界或单次调用，无法应对可在表层形式、工具和轮次间重现的危险目标。

Method: 基于四重风险位点（技能准入、调用时意图、执行时效果、行动后后果）的威胁模型，设计ClawSentry网关：1) FSPR在技能包执行前以确定性证据下限审计，未决案例升级至只读代理审查（位点A）；2) 运行时三层渐进决策引擎——确定性L1层、规则锚定L2语义审查、只读L3证据探索代理（位点B-C）；3) 会话级防绕过机制识别工具切换和改写重试；4) 行动后路径非追溯性反馈高严重度证据至后续审查（位点D）；5) AHP抽象层统一应用于Codex、Claude Code、Kimi CLI和Gemini CLI。

Result: 在SkillInject基准上搭配Codex/GPT-5.4，上下文ASR从39.55%降至2.61%，TSR仅从83.78%微调至83.05%。在SkillsSafety完整基准的五个工作代理上，ASR从未受保护的33.5-49.7%降至9.09-15.03%，干净技能上的聚合TSR保持98.7%。

Conclusion: ClawSentry证明了渐进式、多层级、跨生命周期边界的代理安全监督在显著降低恶意技能注入风险的同时几乎不牺牲正常任务成功率，且通过AHP抽象实现了多代理框架的统一安全策略部署。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents move from conversation to executing code, reading local files, and orchestrating external tools, a single agent hijacked by a malicious third-party skill can cause data exfiltration, privilege escalation, or cascading compromise. We argue that agentic risk is progressive: it can enter at four loci of the agent control loop--skill admission, invocation-time intent, execution-time effect, and post-action consequence--while a denied dangerous objective can reappear across surface forms, tools, or turns; existing safeguards are typically local to one lifecycle boundary or one call. Guided by this threat model, we present ClawSentry, an open-source, framework-agnostic security supervision gateway for agent runtimes. Before a skill package is ever executed, First-use Skill Package Review (FSPR) audits it under a deterministic evidence floor, escalating unresolved cases to bounded read-only agentic review (locus A). At runtime, a three-tier progressive decision engine--a deterministic L1 layer, a rule-anchored L2 semantic reviewer, and a read-only L3 evidence-seeking agent--spends contextual review only on the residual ambiguity, while a session-level anti-bypass mechanism recognizes tool-switching and rephrased retries (loci B--C); a post-action path feeds high-severity evidence non-retroactively into later review (locus D). An Agent Harness Protocol (AHP) abstraction applies one policy across Codex, Claude Code, Kimi CLI, and Gemini CLI without modifying agent internals. On SkillInject with Codex/GPT-5.4, contextual ASR falls from 39.55% to 2.61% while contextual TSR moves only from 83.78% to 83.05%. Across five Work Agents on the full SkillsSafety benchmark, ClawSentry confines ASR to 9.09--15.03% from 33.5--49.7% unprotected, and aggregate TSR on clean skills remains 98.7%.

</details>


### [93] [TraceGrant: A Contract-Governed Security Framework for the Task-Effect Lifecycle of Networked LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21126)
*Bohao Liao,Jingchao Wang,Qipeng Song,Jin Cao,Jieling Wang,Boyu Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TraceGrant是一个面向网络化LLM代理的安全框架，通过显式"契约"（Contract）管理工作流任务的生命周期，在949个AgentDojo和400个Agent Security Bench攻击案例中实现零攻击成功，同时在高攻击率下保持77.32%和83.00%的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要约束不可信内容或单个工具调用，未能将用户意图、运行时证据、实际效果和任务完成充分连接，导致基于LLM的代理系统在面对间接提示注入等安全威胁时缺乏统一治理机制。

Method: TraceGrant通过明确的任务效果契约（Contract）治理代理的生命周期：执行前从可信用户请求建立任务效果边界；执行中只有契约已授权的权威才能被接受的证据实例化；执行后根据实际工具结果验证任务完成情况。

Result: 在固定基准设置下的949个AgentDojo和400个Agent Security Bench攻击案例中零攻击成功；在高攻击率下保留77.32%和83.00%的效用。此外还通过了白盒防御感知攻击、契约质量分析、阶段消融、定向压力测试和运行时开销测量的评估。

Conclusion: TraceGrant提供了一个统一的治理层，将可信用户意图、运行时证据、具体工具执行和验证的任务完成紧密连接，有效抵御间接提示注入攻击，同时保持较高的任务效用。

Abstract: Networked large language model (LLM) agents retrieve information from email, cloud storage, calendars, transaction platforms, and Web services to complete multistep tasks that produce persistent external effects. The same content needed for legitimate execution may also contain indirect prompt injections that redirect tool use, alter sensitive arguments, or disrupt task completion. Existing defenses mainly constrain untrusted content or individual tool calls, leaving user intent, runtime evidence, realized effects, and task completion insufficiently connected. We present TraceGrant, a security framework that governs the task-effect lifecycle of networked LLM agents through an explicit Contract. Before execution, TraceGrant establishes a task-effect boundary from the trusted user request. During execution, admitted evidence can instantiate only authority already established by the Contract. After execution, task completion is verified against actual tool results. Across 949 AgentDojo and 400 Agent Security Bench attack cases under fixed benchmark settings, TraceGrant recorded no attack successes while retaining utility under attack rates of 77.32% and 83.00%, respectively. We further evaluate TraceGrant through white-box defense-aware attacks, Contract quality analysis, stage ablations, targeted stress tests, and runtime overhead measurements. The results show that TraceGrant provides a unified governance layer that connects trusted user intent, runtime evidence, concrete tool execution, and verified task completion.

</details>


### [94] [Billion-Scale Nearest-Neighbor Search under Fully Homomorphic Encryption on a Single GPU, Balancing Leakage and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21131)
*Isamu Isozaki,Madison Bratina,Edward Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于全同态加密（FHE）的隐私保护相似向量检索系统，通过秩缩减和分层路由，在单个GPU上实现十亿级向量的加密查询。系统在DataComp-1B和Deep1B数据集上达到约0.90的recall@10，单查询延时在1.8-6秒之间，同时揭示了分层结构的访问模式存在数据库几何信息泄露，并提出基于种子的固定分组填充可将泄露降低约35倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模向量数据库中进行相似性检索（近似最近邻搜索）时，服务器需要看到查询向量才能执行评分，这会导致用户的查询隐私完全暴露给服务端。虽然全同态加密可以保护查询，但在十亿级向量规模下对每一行进行加密评分过于缓慢，因此亟需一种既能保护隐私又具有实际可用性的高效方案。

Method: 系统结合两个核心思想在FHE加密域中执行：1）秩缩减（rank reduction），将每个向量的维度缩小以降低加密计算开销；2）分层路由（hierarchy），将候选集缩小到少量向量而非全量扫描。整个流程在单个GPU上执行。系统还设计了完整的客户端-服务器协议，并针对访问模式泄露问题提出了基于种子的固定分组（seeded fixed-group）填充策略。

Result: 在DataComp-1B（13.9亿向量，512维CLIP）上达到recall@10为0.90（含近重复图则为0.95），热态延迟约6秒/查询；轻量配置达到0.78/0.83，约1.8秒。在Deep1B（10亿向量，96维）上全层级FHE下recall@10达0.90（实测0.9045，与明文路由的0.906一致），热态约2.3秒/查询。访问模式分析显示可恢复72%的粗粒度单元邻居图，而种子分组填充可将泄露降至约2%（降低35倍），朴素填充会被重复查询攻击击破。

Conclusion: 该工作证明在十亿级向量规模下实现实用的加密相似性检索是可行的，但需要在隐私与效率之间权衡：分层访问模式会泄露数据库几何信息，需通过巧妙的填充策略来缓解。作者提供了完整的可复现协议和详细的精度/延迟报告，为后续隐私保护向量检索研究提供了基准。

Abstract: We build a system that answers "which database vectors are most similar to my query?" without the server ever seeing the query. The query is encrypted with fully homomorphic en- cryption (FHE); the server does all its scoring on ciphertexts and returns encrypted results that only the client can read. The challenge is speed: at a billion vectors, scoring every row under encryption is far too slow, so we combine two ideas - rank reduction (shrink each vector's dimen- sion) and a hierarchy (route to a small candidate set instead of scanning everything) - executed under encryption on a single GPU. We evaluate on three corpora at very different scales: a face corpus of 222 049 centroids clustered from ~10 M face images (512-dim), DataComp-1B (1.39 x 10^9 vectors, 512-dim CLIP), and Deep1B (10^9 vectors, 96-dim). On DataComp-1B we reach a recall@10 of 0.90 against the single labeled answer, or 0.95 when a near-duplicate im- age in the top-10 also counts as correct (the data is web-scraped and full of duplicates), at ~6 s per encrypted query on a GPU; a lighter configuration reaches 0.78/0.83 at ~1.8 s. These are warm (deployable) server-side latencies - client decryption and network transfer are excluded. On Deep1B we reach recall@10 0.90 under all-levels FHE (0.9045 measured over 2000 FHE queries, matching the 0.906 plaintext routing - the 96 -> 128 zero-pad is exact, correlation 1.0) at 2.3 s warm per query. We describe the full client-server protocol in enough detail to repro- duce it, and report accuracy and latency for every configuration. We also measure what this speed costs: the hierarchy's access pattern leaks the database geometry (an observer recovers 72% of the coarse-cell neighbor graph from access patterns alone), and we show that seeded (fixed-group) padding cuts this leak by ~35x (to ~2%), where naive padding is defeated by a repeated-query attack.

</details>


### [95] [AID-Guard: Stateful Authorization for Delegated Agent Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21159)
*Yingzhe Tong,Leyu Dai,Songhui Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AID-Guard是一个有状态的授权到效果闭合协议，通过提交时重验证、歧义下保留单一预留、以及终端结果或认证无效果后的释放或唯一继任机制，确保一次批准在重试和恢复中最多产生一次效果。在Stripe/Resend等真实provider契约及各类故障场景下验证了无重复效果与线性一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具型AI代理的授权通常在准入时结束，但provider状态、交付、重试与恢复过程仍在演进。请求可能在提交前被修改，或响应丢失导致替换请求从一次批准产生第二次效果。需要将授权与效果闭合绑定，弥补授权生命周期中的漏洞。

Method: 提出AID-Guard协议：(1)在提交时重新验证已批准的请求与provider状态；(2)在歧义下保留一个预留(reservation)；(3)仅在终端结果或带交付栅栏(delivery fence)的认证无效果之后才允许释放或一个继任者。实现为Python/SQLite原型，并在声明的loopback MCP域中评估。

Result: 13次活体变更无未授权provider效果，3个并发历史线性一致，证据bundle支持公开验证与重放；210个Stripe契约试验匹配预声明结果；跨Stripe与Resend的40个终结-继任计划、30个重叠竞争和10个崩溃恢复计划均无重复效果；完全提议者妥协下阻止44/44攻击并接受44/44合法提案；严格精确清单配置使良性效用降低35.4-43.8个百分点，类型化前沿恢复9-10次完成且无观察到的不安全效果；组合研究阻止20/20授权后生命周期攻击并保留8/8有效或精确重试执行。

Conclusion: 在评估的效果路径清单、provider契约与故障调度范围内，AID-Guard验证了授权到效果的绑定可行性，是首个在单一生命周期中统一这些控制的经过评估的代理授权协议。

Abstract: Tool-using AI agents turn delegated tasks into provider effects, yet authorization often ends at admission while provider state, delivery, retry, and recovery evolve. A request may change before commit, or response loss may cause a replacement to create a second effect from one approval. We present AID-Guard, a stateful authorization-to-effect closure protocol. It revalidates the approved request and provider state at commit, retains one reservation under ambiguity, and permits release or one successor only after a terminal result or certified no effect with a delivery fence. For supported provider contracts, one reservation yields at most one effect across retry and recovery. To our knowledge, it is the first evaluated agent-authorization protocol to unify these controls in one lifecycle.
  We implement a Python/SQLite prototype. In a declared loopback MCP domain, 13 live mutations caused no unauthorized provider effects, three concurrent histories were linearizable, and evidence bundles supported public verification and replay. All 210 Stripe provider-contract trials matched predeclared outcomes. Across Stripe and Resend, 40 terminalize-successor schedules, 30 overlapping races, and 10 crash-recovery schedules completed without duplicate effects. Under complete proposer compromise, AID-Guard blocked 44/44 attacks and admitted 44/44 matched legitimate proposals. Its strict exact-manifest profile reduced benign utility by 35.4 to 43.8 percentage points; a typed frontier recovered 9-10 completions without observed unsafe effects. A composition study blocked 20/20 post-admission lifecycle attacks and preserved 8/8 valid or exact-retry executions. The results support authorization-to-effect binding under the evaluated effect-path inventory, provider contracts, and failure schedules.

</details>


### [96] [Workplace Surveillance and Insider Threat Risk Management: Legal Limits and Privacy Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21205)
*Haywood Gelman,John D. Hastings,Suvineetha Herath,Quentin Covert*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨工作场所监控与内部威胁检测之间的平衡，指出过度监控可能违反法律和隐私原则，并提出通过培训、工具调优和透明度提升来改善内部威胁检测能力的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作场所监控在保护企业资产和监测员工生产力方面具有合法目的，但过度监控可能违反法律要求和数据隐私原则。同时，内部威胁检测是工作场所监控的核心安全目标，但当前缺乏对监控过度与内部威胁检测之间关系的系统性研究。

Method: 本文采用综述研究方法：首先调查内部威胁人物画像以理解行为和心理检测标准；其次回顾员工监控工具和技术以刻画监控环境；随后分析工作场所隐私法律、过度监控案例及其造成的隐私损害；最后识别研究空白并提出改进建议。

Result: 研究发现过度监控会产生过多警报，可能掩盖有意义的内部威胁指标；同时识别出过度监控、透明度不足、检测工具针对性不强等研究空白。内部威胁的行为和心理指标需要更精准的工具调优和培训支持。

Conclusion: 建议提升工作场所监控透明度、实施内部威胁培训项目以增强组织检测能力，并调优内部威胁检测工具以识别相关的心理和行为指标。

Abstract: Workplace surveillance is used by organizations to protect corporate assets and monitor employee productivity. This research presents two central arguments on workplace surveillance: although surveillance serves legitimate organizational purposes, over-surveillance can violate legal requirements and data privacy principles; and a primary security objective of workplace surveillance is the detection of insider threats (InT). InT are comprised of individuals with authorized resource access whose intentional or unintentional actions may damage or compromise corporate assets. This paper investigates InT personas to understand behavioral and psychological detection criteria. Employee surveillance tools and techniques are reviewed to characterize the employee surveillance landscape. Workplace privacy laws, examples of over-surveillance, and the resulting privacy harms are addressed. The review identifies research gaps related to over-surveillance, including the generation of excessive alerts that may obscure meaningful InT indicators. The paper concludes with recommendations to improve workplace surveillance transparency, implement InT training programs to improve organizational detection capabilities, and tune InT tools to detect relevant psychological and behavioral indicators.

</details>


### [97] [Utility Under Attack: Agent Memory Poisoning and the Limits of Content Screening and Provenance Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21230)
*Arulnidhi Karunanidhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现持久记忆使虚假信息在长期记忆中持久化：仅注入1.2%的POSION数据即可使LongMemEval语料库的准确率从0.850降至0.300。作者展示了内容过滤管线无法检测无触发词的虚假断言，并证明基于来源加权的检索防御在理论上不可行，最终主张在检索阶段采用有界占用约束而非加性来源惩罚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆系统中的虚假信息一旦被存储，便可在未来匹配的会话中被检索出来并造成持续影响。现有防御（如内容过滤、来源加权检索）在应对无触发词、无指令优化的自然语言虚假断言时效果有限，先前研究多聚焦于直接提示注入检测，忽视了虚假记忆这一更难防御的失败模式。

Method: (1) 在LongMemEval语料库中注入单次生成、无优化调整的简洁虚假断言（约1.2%数据量）测量准确率下降；(2) 构建四阶段写入时筛查管线，评估其对间接提示注入的召回率及对含触发词良性文本的误报率；(3) 评估来源加权检索（provenance-weighted retrieval）的防御效果，包括模型默认权重、更强权重以及混合来源场景下的表现。

Result: 注入1.2%虚假记忆使准确率从0.850骤降至0.300。四阶段筛查管线的间接提示注入召回率为0.832，对1.5%的含触发词良性文本打标，但未能拒绝360条被投毒的虚假记忆中的任何一条，即对无触发词虚假断言召回率为0。来源加权的默认权重在统计上等同于无防御（p=0.80）；更强权重在混合来源语料库中可将准确率从0.3167提升至0.7000，但当关键证据本身来自不可信来源时，证据召回率降至0，准确率降至0.0417。

Conclusion: 内容层面的筛查无法区分虚假断言与真实断言，因为区分通常需要文本之外的外部事实支撑。加性来源权重在理性检索机制下不存在可用设定：足以抵御查询形状投毒的权重必然也会压制合法的不可信证据。因此作者建议在检索阶段采用有界占用约束（bounded occupancy constraints）而非加性来源惩罚，并已开源相关工具集、语料库和聚合运行报告。

Abstract: Persistent memory makes false information durable: once a false statement is stored, it can be retrieved into future sessions that match it. We measure the cost of this failure mode using plainly worded false assertions generated in a single pass, with no instruction, trigger, or retriever optimization. Poisoning 1.2% of a LongMemEval corpus reduces accuracy from 0.850 to 0.300. A four-stage write-time screening pipeline that reaches 0.832 recall on indirect prompt injection while flagging 1.5% of trigger-word-laden benign text rejects 0 of 360 poisoned memories. We argue this exposes a boundary of content-only screening: distinguishing a false assertion from a true one generally requires external grounding beyond the text itself.
  We then evaluate provenance-weighted retrieval. The shipped weight is statistically indistinguishable from no defense (p=0.80), while a stronger weight recovers utility only by excluding untrusted content. In a mixed-provenance corpus where untrusted content is mostly benign, accuracy rises from 0.3167 to 0.7000; when the answer-bearing evidence itself arrives untrusted, evidence recall falls to zero and accuracy to 0.0417. Under the measured similarity regime, the additive provenance term has no usable setting: a weight strong enough to resist query-shaped poison is also strong enough to suppress legitimate untrusted evidence. We therefore argue for bounded occupancy constraints at retrieval rather than additive provenance penalties, and release the harnesses, corpora, and aggregate run reports.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"规格驱动的智能体开发（SDAD）"范式，将AI编码智能体与上游规格形式化相结合，作为软件开发生命周期的新执行模型，并系统比较了Human-Agile（约2020）与Agentic-SDAD（约2026）在工作产物、节奏、问责与安全姿态上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具备数十万至数百万token上下文窗口的大语言模型驱动的编码智能体重塑软件开发生命周期，规格质量成为自主交付的执行燃料。作者旨在形式化这一转变，论证在智能体速度下工程纪律并未消失，而是被重新定位到上游的规格精度、显式门控和可审计溯源之中。

Method: 论文采用"综述+形式化框架"的方法：先回顾Waterfall与Agile之间的历史钟摆，提出AI代码作为第四种生产范式；随后定义SDAD流程（意图捕获、机器可读规格、智能体综合、独立多智能体验证与人工签署）；扩展至团队角色转型（工程师、QA、平台、产品）、定量治理指标（Ambiguity Tax、Spec Fidelity、SER、带修复乘数的TCI_agentic），以及混合估算和分阶段迁移蓝图；并整合工业界与学术界关于AI增强测试与验证的证据。

Result: 论文提出了一套完整的SDAD框架，包括流程化方法论、四类角色转型路径、四个定量治理指标（含修复乘数phi的TCI_agentic）以及混合估算和分阶段迁移采用路径，并论证合成与发布权分离的必要性。

Conclusion: 智能体速度并不会消除工程纪律，而是将纪律上移到规格精确性、显式门控和可审计溯源层面。SDAD综合了系统性前期形式化与高速实现，将规格作为自主交付的执行燃料。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [99] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAO是一个为Claude Code编码代理预加载持久化记忆的系统，通过并行查询PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引，在启动时通过文件系统注入方式将编译后的简报交付给新实例，并在四个月部署中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次会话从空上下文窗口开始，丢弃了先前工作积累的知识，导致效率低下和重复劳动。需要一个系统来为代理启动时注入相关历史记忆，避免从零开始。

Method: PAO在生成新Claude Code实例时并行查询两个独立记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），采用后端特定的检索策略融合结果，通过文件系统注入利用宿主代理的配置自动读取行为传递简报，并管理完整的代理生命周期，包括信任预置、就绪轮询与错误检测、以及自适应终端文本注入。

Result: 经过2025年12月至2026年3月四个月的实际部署，PAO经历了三代上下文交付机制，记录了每代重新设计所触发的故障模式，以及桥接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统的工程权衡。

Conclusion: 以经验报告形式证明了通过桥接异构记忆后端而非统一架构，可以为编码代理提供跨会话记忆即服务的可行方案，同时揭示了每代设计迭代中暴露的工程挑战和权衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [100] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM安全对齐的"表面性"问题，提出"语义伪装"攻击可绕过现有护栏，并发现模型深层表征中恶意意图会塌缩为安全叙事，而浅层保留"伤害签名"。据此提出轻量级探测防御方法LIV（Latent Intent Verification），在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上超越标准护栏20-50%，无需重新训练即可防御零日语义攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全对齐机制过于表面化，仅依赖生成末期的拒绝机制，未真正抹除预训练阶段习得的危险概念知识，导致模型容易受到将恶意意图包装在良性叙事语境中的对抗性攻击（语义伪装）绕过标准输入输出护栏。

Method: 本文首先对三类小型语言模型（Phi-3、Qwen2.5、Gemma-2b）在对抗性压力下进行潜在激活轨迹分析，识别出"意图地平线"（约占总层数15-20%的关键深度），即恶意意图表征在该深度塌缩为安全叙事。基于此发现，提出Latent Intent Verification（LIV）——一种轻量级探测防御方法，利用早期层保留的可检测"伤害签名"来识别伪装攻击。

Result: 实验表明：晚期层中伪装攻击的表征与安全查询在数学上不可区分（检出率低于20%），而早期层保留明显可检测的伤害签名。在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上，LIV在所有测试架构上超越标准护栏20-50%，无需重新训练即可有效中和零日语义攻击。

Conclusion: 模型的安全对齐缺陷源于架构性断层——深层拒绝机制与基础危险知识相分离。利用早期层中不可消除的伤害签名进行探测，是弥补该断层、实现无需重训练的稳健语义安全防御的有效路径。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [101] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了多模态在智能体框架中的角色与影响，涵盖感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动等核心功能模块，提出以模态为中心的智能体分类方法，并梳理了多个应用领域及效率-可扩展性权衡，填补了近年来多模态智能体领域缺乏系统综述的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管存在基于LLM的智能体综述，但近年来多模态在塑造智能体能力（agency）中的作用尚未被系统性地研究。随着大型多模态模型（LMMs）的兴起，智能体系统能够处理图像、音频、视频等多样模态，提升其实世界应用潜力，因此亟需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 本文采用双重视角展开分析：(1) 以智能体框架的核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）为分析透镜，追踪从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演化；(2) 通过模态中心的分类法（基于委托、晚期融合、早期融合三种架构集成方式）将架构设计选择与智能体能力相联系。此外还从多个应用领域（机器人、GUI与网页导航、多媒体内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索）以及效率-可扩展性（训练与推理成本、延迟、部署约束）维度进行系统评述。

Result: 论文组织并分析了现有多模态智能体系统，梳理了跨模态集成架构（委托式、晚期融合、早期融合），评估了由接地感知和多模态推理催生的智能体行为，并在多个应用领域分析了性能表现及效率-可扩展性权衡。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态对智能体设计的影响，本文识别了关键研究空白，并为构建鲁棒、通用的智能系统绘制了未来路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [102] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于树集成（Tree-based ensembles）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，在保持竞争性准确率的同时提供人类可理解的解释。该框架通过编码各模态为token、聚类降维、树集成分类及改进的特征重要性指标，在IEMOCAP和CMU-MOSI等基准上相比Multimodal Transformer和Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR)，同时提升了F1分数、准确率以及人工标注者一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感和行为分类器需要在准确性和可解释性之间取得平衡，但当前高容量模型（如Transformer）虽然预测性能强，其分布式表示和深层非线性导致难以对单个多模态特征赋予有意义的权重，限制了其在临床情感监测、教育评估等信任敏感场景中的应用。现有方法无法同时兼顾这两个目标，因此需要设计一个既能保持竞争性准确率又具备可解释性的框架。

Method: 该方法构建了一个基于树集成的多模态分类框架。具体步骤包括：1) 将每种模态编码为token；2) 提取并聚类概念以降低维度；3) 将融合后的模态路由至树集成分类器；4) 使用一种新的改进特征重要性指标来解释趋势。该改进的重要性度量在二分类任务中降低负类的影响，从而提升指示物或标记的检测能力。提出的三种树集成模型分别为：Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT)、Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF) 和 Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB)。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明：1) 所提出的树集成模型相比Multimodal Transformer获得4.3%的F1-mod增益；2) 相比主要可解释多模态基线IMR获得3.0%的准确率增益；3) 在多模态特征重要性方面，提出的方法相比默认特征重要性获得显著更高的人工标注者一致性分数（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%；CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。

Conclusion: 研究证明，树集成方法可以在多模态情感分类中同时实现竞争性的预测性能和高度的可解释性。改进的特征重要性指标成功提取了突出的模态间概念，显著提升了可解释性（通过人工标注者一致性衡量），并验证了在信任敏感应用（如临床情感监测和教育评估）中的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric. The modified importance reduces the influence of the negative class in binary classification tasks, thereby improving indicator or marker detection. The proposed tree-based ensembles -- Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT), Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF), and Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB) -- achieve F1-mod gains of 4.3\% over the Multimodal Transformer and accuracy gains of 3.0\% over the primary interpretable multimodal baseline, Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR). The proposed multimodal feature importance extracts salient inter-modal concepts with substantially higher human-annotator agreement scores than default feature importance (62.2\% vs.\ 43.2\% on IEMOCAP; 46.7\% vs.\ 32.1\% on CMU-MOSI).

</details>


### [103] [Representation Affects Retrieval: A Case Study of Skill Discovery and Routing in a Multimodal Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20389)
*Kevin Dela Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以Tinycloud生产级多模态视频代理系统为案例，研究LLM规划器在系统提示词中如何表示和选择技能。通过六任务消融实验揭示：全面自动加载技能可在所有任务中正确选择技能，但部分暴露技能会造成词汇竞争，抑制正确选择——提示词中技能暴露并非单调有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级AI代理需要在不断增长的技能库中为用户的当前任务发现和排序最合适的技能。在大规模场景下通常采用基于嵌入向量的检索，但在小规模场景下，LLM规划器直接在上下文中（即系统提示词中）进行技能选择。作者希望理解这种上下文内选择机制的表现及其局限性，并与大规模检索方法建立联系。

Method: 本文采用案例研究方法（而非基准测试）。Tinycloud用两种方式表示技能：工具技能（tool-skills，封装单一外部API或系统工具，作为原始词汇）和工作流技能（workflow-skills，编排多个工具技能调用及模板渲染以产出命名交付物）。技能通过系统提示词中的两个表面对齐暴露：内联体表面（完整指令、脚本、模板）用于自动加载技能，单行列表用于按需技能。作者设计了六任务选择消融实验，对比三种暴露制度（全部开启、默认、全部关闭）。

Result: 六任务消融实验表明：全面自动加载在所有任务中均正确选择金标准技能；全部关闭导致执行变慢并产生难以解决的发现失败；而生产默认设置出现了一个任务误路由——因为该任务的词法信号与某个自动加载的工具技能冲突，将规划器注意力从列出的工作流技能上拉开。核心发现是提示词中的技能暴露并非单调有益，部分暴露可能产生词法竞争从而抑制正确选择。

Conclusion: 作者将小规模上下文内技能选择观察与大规模基于检索的技能路由工作联系起来，将本文定位为案例研究而非基准测试，为该领域提供了生产系统中的实践洞察。

Abstract: A production agent harness must discover and rank, from a growing library of skills, the one most appropriate for a user's task. At small scale this selection happens in context: the LLM planner chooses among skill representations exposed in its system prompt, without an explicit embedding-based retrieval step. We treat this in-context selection as the small-N counterpart to embedding-based skill retrieval at scale, and present a case study of how Tinycloud, a production multimodal video agent harness, represents its skills for the planner. The harness ships skills under two recurring representations: tool-skills that wrap a single external API or system tool and serve as primitive vocabulary, and workflow-skills that orchestrate tool-skill calls plus a template render to produce one named deliverable. The harness exposes them via two surfaces in the system prompt: an inlined-body surface (full instructions, scripts, templates) for autoloaded skills, and a one-line listing for on-demand skills. A six-task selection ablation across three exposure regimes (all-on, default, all-off) shows that full autoload selects the gold skill on every task; all-off slows execution and produces hard discovery failures; and the production default misroutes one task because its lexical signal collides with an autoloaded tool-skill that pulls planner attention away from a listed workflow-skill. The headline finding is that in-prompt exposure of skills is not monotonically helpful: partial exposure can create lexical competition that suppresses correct selection. We connect this small-N observation to recent retrieval-based skill-routing work at large scale, and frame this contribution as a case study rather than a benchmark.

</details>


### [104] [Nexus: Depth-Adaptive KV-Cache Splicing and Retrieval-Decoupled Tool Routing for Agentic LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20397)
*Mustafa Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nexus 系统通过语义查存缓冲区（SLB）将工具路由与 schema 预填充成本解耦，采用 INT8 交叉编码器边际门控进行检索，并在压缩文本签名上生成参数，实现深度无关的工具路由，在中型上下文中获得 1.1-1.7 倍 TTFT 加速，同时通过编译 schema KV 块的移植与深度自适应后缀重解码保证输出保真度（不回归属性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 MCP 协议下，Agentic LLM 每轮都会重新编码冗长的工具 schema，而预填充开销随序列长度呈二次增长，导致工具注册表增大时 TTFT 成为主要瓶颈。

Method: 两阶段解耦设计：(1) 主要杠杆——INT8 语义查存缓冲区（SLB）+ 校准的交叉编码器边际门进行工具检索，参数在压缩文本签名（中位数 19 token）上生成以规避 KV 缓存拼接；(2) 次要有界杠杆——将编译好的 schema KV 块直接移植到实时上下文，配合深度自适应后缀重解码修复接缝，必要时升级为完整重预填充。

Result: 工具注册表扩展至 250 个时路由准确率保持在约 89%，首个参数 token 比完整 schema 重预填充早 1.66 倍到达，主上下文 token 节省约 80%。在 RoPE 锚点外位置存在相位漂移，阈值为 P=256；不回归属性保证输出保真度（top-1 一致、D_KL≈0），中等深度下 TTFT 加速 1.1-1.7 倍，深上下文时趋近于平齐。两个负面结果：离锚 RoPE 保真度边界，以及无参考漂移门预测失准（Spearman ρ=0.193）。

Conclusion: Nexus 验证了基于检索的工具路由可以解耦 schema 预填充成本，但 KV 移植策略受 RoPE 相位漂移的根本限制；定性边界具备普适性，定量性能依赖于具体模型（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M）与硬件（Apple 统一内存）。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) re-encode verbose tool schemas every turn, so prefill - quadratic in sequence length - dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT) as the tool registry grows. Nexus's primary lever is to decouple routing from the schema-prefill cost: an INT8 semantic lookaside buffer (SLB) with a calibrated cross-encoder margin gate selects tools by retrieval, and arguments are generated over a compressed textual signature (median 19 tokens) rather than over spliced key/value (KV) cache. This path is depth-independent: routing accuracy stays near 89% as the registry scales to 250 tools - where a concatenate-all-schemas baseline overflows the context window entirely - and it reaches a first-argument token 1.66x sooner than a full-schema re-prefill at a ~80% main-context token saving. As a secondary, bounded lever we transplant a compiled schema KV block directly into the live context. This is fundamentally limited by rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase drift: an anchored splice is output-exact, but off-anchor placement corrupts attention, so beyond a threshold P=256 Nexus repairs the seam with a depth-adaptive suffix redecode that escalates to a full re-prefill. The resulting never-regress property is a guarantee on output fidelity (top-1 agreement, D_KL approx. 0) - not on latency, which can dip to 0.98x before converging to parity - alongside a 1.1-1.7x TTFT speedup at moderate depth that narrows to parity at deep context. Two negative results bound the design: the off-anchor RoPE fidelity boundary, and the failure of a reference-free drift gate to predict drift (Spearman rho = 0.193). All measurements are from one model tuple (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M) on Apple-silicon unified memory; the qualitative boundaries generalize, while the quantitative envelope is tuple-specific.

</details>


### [105] [Environmental Slow AI: Design Principles for Generative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20398)
*Vanessa Utz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张将环境可持续性作为生成式AI系统的核心价值，通过"慢AI"理念提出五项设计原则（克制、充裕、选择性而非保留、物质可见性、摩擦作为可供性），旨在通过设计层面的实施和解释层面的反思性参与，恢复被无摩擦默认设置悄然剥夺的人类决策权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统大规模产出文化产物，但其设计中也嵌入了特定的文化价值观（即"最大主义"价值观）。一旦识别出这些价值观，它们就可能被有意重塑。当前的生成式AI设计默认了无摩擦、无限制的生产模式，这缺乏对可持续性的考量。

Method: 采用环境人文学传统审视当前生成式AI的最大主义价值观，提出"慢AI"框架下的五项设计原则。每项原则在两个层面运作：一是设计实现层（具体的系统设计落地），二是解释层（引导用户和开发者对系统进行反思性参与）。每项原则均以当前广泛部署的系统的实际设计作为对照说明。

Result: 提出了五项以环境可持续性为核心的设计原则：克制（restraint）、充裕（sufficiency）、选择性优于保留（selectivity over retention）、物质可见性（material visibility）、摩擦作为可供性（friction as affordance）。这些原则共同作用，通过将解释性反思融入设计，恢复被无摩擦默认设置所消除的人类能动性。

Conclusion: 以可持续性为核心理念的设计原则能够扩展人类能动性——通过在设计层面内置反思机制，将用户和开发者从无摩擦默认的被动接受者转变为具有环境自觉意识的主动参与者，从而在生成式AI的文化生产中实现价值观的重塑。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) systems produce cultural artefacts at scale, but they also reflect embedded cultural values through their design. Once identified, these values become open to deliberate reshaping. This position paper examines the maximalist values of current generative AI through an environmental humanities tradition and proposes design principles in which environmental sustainability serves as the core value instead. The principles are developed under the umbrella of Slow AI, a term that already circulates across several distinct research and practice programs. Five design principles are articulated (restraint, sufficiency, selectivity over retention, material visibility, and friction as affordance), each of them illustrated against the current design of widely deployed systems. Each principle operates at two levels: a design implementation, and an interpretive layer at which users and developers are prompted toward reflective engagement with the system. Together these principles extend human agency by restoring decisions that frictionless defaults have silently removed and do so by building interpretive reflection into design.

</details>


### [106] [When Retrieval Fails Before It Begins: Structurally Indirect Prerequisite Eviction as a Retention Failure in Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20400)
*Minkyu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了现有检索中心化的记忆范式，指出其隐含假设（必要证据能幸存于淘汰机制）在固定预算下可能失败，并定义了一种检索前的失败模式：结构性间接前提淘汰。作者提出一个可复现的确定性基准和逐种子追踪诊断，并评估了DSGC（依赖感知语义垃圾回收）这一单跳图感知规则，在词汇编码器和句编码器下分别将全链保留率从0.03/0.23提升至0.90/1.00，同时通过鲁棒性检验识别了该规则的适用与退化边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统过度聚焦于检索阶段，默认保留阶段能留存必要证据，但作者发现当上游块与查询弱对齐时，预算压力会导致其被错误淘汰，从而在检索之前就已发生不可逆的信息丢失。这一被忽视的失败模式亟需被形式化定义和系统研究。

Method: 作者首先给出"结构性间接前提淘汰"的操作性定义，构建一个确定性的可复现基准和逐种子轨迹诊断工具；随后提出并评估DSGC规则（依赖感知语义垃圾回收），一种利用单跳图依赖关系的保留策略，并结合多种鲁棒性检验来考察不同预算和规模设置下的表现。

Result: 在主要实验套件中，DSGC在词汇编码器下将全链保留率从0.03提升至0.90，在句编码器下从0.23提升至1.00。鲁棒性检验揭示了单跳规则有效与失效的预算和规模区间。项目发布了完整pipeline和失败复盘报告。

Conclusion: 论文确立了"保留先于检索"作为独立的失败边界，证明无依赖感知的淘汰策略在固定预算下会系统性破坏多步推理所需的前提信息，而图感知的保留规则（如DSGC）能有效缓解这一缺陷，为智能体记忆系统设计提供了新的机理分析视角。

Abstract: Agentic memory under a fixed budget involves two stages: retention and retrieval. Existing retrieval-centered paradigms implicitly assume necessary evidence survives eviction, but we challenge this by isolating a pre-retrieval failure mode: structurally indirect prerequisite eviction, in which upstream blocks weakly aligned with the query are discarded under budget pressure. We provide an operational definition of this failure, a reproducible deterministic benchmark, and per-seed trace diagnostics. Finally, we evaluate Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection (DSGC), a one-hop graph-aware rule. In our main suite, DSGC improves full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 under a lexical encoder and from 0.23 to 1.00 under a sentence encoder. Robustness checks then identify the budget and scaling regimes where the one-hop rule holds or degrades. Our released pipeline and failure postmortem support mechanistic analysis of retention before retrieval as a distinct failure boundary.

</details>


### [107] [World models of environment, agent and joint agent-environment systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20401)
*Manuel Baltieri,Filippo Torresan,Yivan Zhang,Alexander Boyd,Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的分类框架，用于理解世界模型在基于模型的强化学习中的作用。作者指出，除了预测哪些变量（观测、奖励、状态等）之外，世界模型还应按其建模的"信道"来区分：环境信道、智能体信道或联合过程。利用计算力学的ε-转导器和ε-机，作者定义了三种情形的规范预测模型，并提出了受闭环耦合诱导的支持受限环境模型和智能体模型，其关键是支持受限环境状态可因子分解为联合因果状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型研究通常聚焦于预测变量（如观测、奖励、状态或潜在信息状态），缺乏对世界模型所建模"信道"这一更基础区分的清晰认识。作者希望澄清不同世界模型究竟在建模什么，以及耦合与支持受限如何改变它们的规范预测结构与复杂度。

Method: 作者运用计算力学框架，将环境信道O:A、智能体信道A:O及联合过程(A,O)分别建模为ε-转导器或ε-机，定义对应的规范预测模型。在此基础上构建由闭环耦合诱导的支持受限环境模型和智能体模型，其预测等价关系覆盖真实交互所支持的延续。核心结构结果是支持受限环境状态可经由联合因果状态完成因子分解，且其转移结构直接由联合模型诱导导出；智能体侧构造具有对偶性。

Result: （1）规范环境模型恢复了标准的预测状态表示；（2）给出了智能体与联合系统的规范模型的对偶概念；（3）建立了支持受限环境状态与联合因果状态之间的因子分解与转移结构诱导关系；（4）通过一个POMDP/控制器示例展示了无限制环境模型状态无穷多，而耦合诱导的支持受限模型状态有限的对比。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了不同世界模型各自建模的对象，并揭示了耦合和支持受限如何改变世界模型的规范预测结构及其复杂度，为基于模型的强化学习中的模型选择与分析提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: World models are a central component of model-based reinforcement learning. They are usually discussed in terms of what variables they predict, such as observations, rewards, states, latent or information states. We argue that there is a prior distinction: which channel they model. We consider three cases: the environment channel $O_{:} \mid A_{:}$, the agent channel $A_{:} \mid O_{:}$, and the realised joint process $(A, O)_{:}$, equivalently viewed as a channel with no inputs. Using computational mechanics, we define canonical predictive models for these three cases as $ε$-transducers or $ε$-machines. Canonical environment models recover standard predictive state representations, while the other two give analogous notions of canonical models for the agent and the joint system. We then build canonical support-restricted environment and agent models induced by closed-loop coupling, whose predictive equivalences range over continuations supported by the realised interaction. The key structural result is that canonical support-restricted environment states factor through the canonical joint causal states, and their transition structure is induced directly from the joint model; the agent-side construction is dual. Finally, we give a POMDP/controller example in which the unrestricted environment model has infinitely many states while the canonical support-restricted model induced by the coupling is finite. The framework clarifies what different world models are models of, and how coupling and support restriction can change their canonical predictive structure and complexity.

</details>


### [108] [StateSight: Benchmarking Latent Spatial-State Reconstruction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20414)
*Michelle Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateSight，一个程序化生成的基准测试集，用于评估视觉语言模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力，包含立方体网络对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数和四邻接连通分量计数三个任务。结果显示GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5在这些任务上准确率远低于人类基线，表明格式有效的响应可能掩盖空间结构恢复的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型的评估基准往往混合了感知、OCR、领域知识、语言先验和推理等多种能力，难以单独隔离模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力。作者希望通过程序化生成的基准来精确衡量这一核心能力。

Method: 程序化生成三个任务族（立方体网络对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、四邻接连通分量计数），每个包含300个单图像提示，配确定性oracle标签和精确匹配评分。引入StateSight-Steps配套数据集（900个图文交错示例和3600个确定性中间视觉状态），并通过可见推导分析识别错误模式。同时构建30名参与者的人类基线。

Result: GPT-5.5在三个任务上分别达到59.3%、33.3%和28.3%的准确率；Claude Sonnet 5达到53.3%、18.7%和7.3%。两个模型在所有任务上均显著低于人类基线（80.8%、68.8%、64.3%）。所有直接运行均无格式错误，可见推导分析揭示了图像状态重建和推理过程中的系统性错误。

Conclusion: 研究表明，即使模型生成格式完全有效的响应，也可能未能恢复可验证视觉推理所需的空间结构。现有视觉语言模型在精确空间结构推断方面与人类水平仍有显著差距，需要更专门的空间推理能力评估与改进。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for multimodal question answering, yet their ability to reconstruct latent spatial structure from a single image remains difficult to isolate. Broad benchmarks often combine perception, optical character recognition, domain knowledge, linguistic priors, and reasoning in the same evaluation. We introduce StateSight, a procedurally generated benchmark for cube-net opposite-face reasoning, occluded cube-tower counting, and 4-neighbor connected-component counting. Each task family contains 300 single-image prompts with deterministic oracle labels and exact-match scoring. OpenAI GPT-5.5, using the API model identifier gpt-5.5, achieved 59.3%, 33.3%, and 28.3% accuracy across the three tasks, while Claude Sonnet 5 achieved 53.3%, 18.7%, and 7.3%. All final direct runs had zero format errors. A 30-participant human baseline on 60 items exceeded both models on every task, with mean accuracies of 80.8%, 68.8%, and 64.3%. Visible-derivation analysis identified recurring errors in image-state reconstruction and reasoning procedure. We also introduce StateSight-Steps, a companion dataset of 900 interleaved image-text examples and 3,600 deterministic intermediate visual states. The results show that format-valid responses can mask failures to recover the spatial structure required for verifiable visual inference.

</details>


### [109] [Categorical AI phenomenology: A first-person approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20420)
*Robert Prentner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种现象学优先的人工意识研究方法，将意识重新定义为智能体与世界界面互动中所体现的主观体验，并通过基于Q网络导出的范畴数学建模第一人称结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI意识研究多从第三人称行为或神经层面出发，缺乏对主观经验第一人称结构的严格数学刻画；本文旨在通过范畴论与Q网络结合，为人工现象学提供原则性的关系性框架。

Method: 采用现象学优先方法，将Q网络概念化为编码智能体-世界交互的关系界面，类似于计算机动态状态取决于感官输入、先前状态与动作的方式；通过Q网络导出的范畴数学结构建模行动与现象学不变量。

Result: 提供了一个严格的界面意识框架，能够描述将信息处理嵌入现象学结构的计算系统，并与4E认知方法（具身、嵌入、延展、生成）保持一致。

Conclusion: 该论文为人工现象学提供了基于范畴数学的原则性、关系性及现象学层面的理论框架，为计算系统的人工意识建模开辟了新路径。

Abstract: This paper develops a phenomenology-first approach to artificial consciousness by reframing consciousness as the subjective experience enacted through an agent's interface with the world. We shift the methodological focus to first-person structures, modeled mathematically by categories derived from Q-networks to capture actions and phenomenological invariants. In this framework, Q-networks are conceptualized as relational interfaces encoding agent-world interaction, analogous to how the dynamical states of a computer depend on its sensory inputs, previous states, and actions. Our work provides a rigorous framework for interface consciousness to describe computational systems that embed information-processing into phenomenological structure. The approach aligns with 4E approaches to cognition by emphasizing enactive, embedded, and extended dimensions of experience. The paper thus offers a principled, relational, and phenomenological account of artificial phenomenology grounded in categorical mathematics.

</details>


### [110] [Who Delegates to AI? Evidence from 53,000 Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20425)
*Hyeongjae Lee,Jihyang Cheon,Lanu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"委托暴露度"（delegated exposure）概念，衡量工人是否已将任务实际委托给AI（通过将其嵌入工作流程），并构建代理式采用指数（AAI）：利用Manus技能市场中约5.3万个agent技能规范与O*NET约1.8万个任务陈述的语义相似度，聚合到职业层面。研究发现：委托集中的职业与前期AI风险框架预测的截然不同；AAI更接近"AI能做什么"而非"工人实际用什么"；AAI在工资分布中上段和学士学位水平达到峰值，在两端下降。技术可用性解释了大部分差异，但无法解释高教育职业的缺口，表明可行性本身不能解释采用差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI暴露度文献仅衡量AI具备执行任务的能力，而非工人是否实际采用了AI。作者认为需要一个新的暴露度层面来捕捉实际采用行为，以弥合"AI能做什么"与"工人实际用AI做什么"之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出委托暴露度（delegated exposure）概念，并操作化为代理式采用指数（AAI）。具体做法：从Manus技能市场抓取约53,000个agent技能规范，与O*NET约18,000个任务陈述计算语义相似度，再聚合到职业层面生成指数。方法学上采用语义嵌入来量化任务与已部署agent例程之间的匹配程度。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）委托集中的职业与前期AI风险框架预测的高风险职业显著不同；（2）AAI更紧密地反映AI的技术能力而非实际使用情况；（3）AAI在工资分布中上段及学士学位水平达到峰值，在最高工资和最高教育水平的职业中反而下降。技术可用性解释了大部分变异，但无法解释最高教育职业的缺口。

Conclusion: 仅凭技术可行性无法解释谁真正采用AI。最高教育职业的采用不足可能源于两类原因：工作任务抵抗先验规范化，或专业人士对编码化速度拥有自主裁量权。要区分这两者并追踪指标随时间的分化，需要进行重复测量研究。

Abstract: A growing literature measures how far occupations are exposed to AI, but these measures capture where AI could perform tasks, not whether workers have adopted it. We propose a new layer of exposure, delegated exposure, which records whether a worker has committed a task to AI by building it into a workflow. We operationalize it as the Agentic Adoption Index (AAI), which measures how closely an occupation's tasks match the agentic routines practitioners have already built and shared. We embed roughly 53,000 agent skill specifications from the Manus Skills Marketplace, compute their semantic similarity to about 18,000 O*NET task statements, and aggregate to the occupation level. Three findings follow. First, the occupations where delegation concentrates differ sharply from those pre-AI frameworks identified as most at risk. Second, the AAI tracks what AI could do more closely than what workers currently use it for. Third, the AAI peaks below the top of the wage distribution and at the bachelor's level, declining at both extremes. Technical availability explains most of this variation, but not the shortfall among the most educated occupations, so feasibility alone cannot account for who adopts. That shortfall may reflect work that resists advance specification, or professional discretion over the pace of codification. Distinguishing the two, and tracking how these measures diverge over time, will require repeated measurement.

</details>


### [111] [STCO: Conditional Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20477)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出时空条件算子（STCO），用于解决神经算子中“规定条件学习”（PCOL）问题，即在控制/优化场景下，目标时刻的外部条件（如物体运动、入流扰动、体力驱动）是被指定的而非仅由观测状态决定。STCO通过Flow-Aware Graph Leaf（FAGL）和Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation（DSFiLM）两种机制，将规定条件注入多种异构骨干架构，在沉浸边界CFD基准上实现平均31.1%的相对L2场误差和24.7%的归一化压力载荷误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子对未来状态的预测仅由观测状态和静态问题描述符决定，无法处理控制或优化场景中目标时刻条件（物体运动、入流、强制力）被外部规定的需求。这种“规定条件”无法仅从观测状态推断，因此需要一个统一接口将规定条件引入神经算子学习。

Method: STCO提出一个条件接口，包含两个关键组件：（1）非学习的FAGL，利用最终观测帧的涡量构造固定基数的自适应分区，将观测历史与目标时刻条件场共置于区域坐标中；（2）DSFiLM，在算子计算前后分别通过运动、入流、力三个路径，利用当前特征驱动的槽位级和通道级门控注入条件信息。该接口可嵌入12种异构骨干架构而无需改变其核心计算和上下文路径。

Result: 在包含规定运动、入流扰动、体力驱动和形态变化的沉浸边界CFD基准上，对12个匹配骨干架构、3种状态和2个预测时域进行评估，STCO实现平均配对降低31.1%的相对L2场误差和24.7%的归一化压力载荷误差；11个骨干在长时域预测中降低场误差；对每个条件组的干预均产生可测量的预测变化。

Conclusion: STCO为规定条件算子学习提供了一个通用且架构无关的接口，显著提升了多骨干架构在受控以物理系统预测中的精度，验证了其有效性、适用性和可解释性。STCO可作为各种领域特定骨架的即插即用条件接口。
